<p class='hn-title'>(1) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: I replaced a $120k bowling center system with $1,600 in ESP32s</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：用 1,600 美元的 ESP32 替代 12 万美元的保龄球馆系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2881</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 343</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An engineer documents swapping a $120k bowling center control system for an ESP32-based setup costing about $1.6k. The project demonstrates how commodity microcontrollers and open-source software can replace expensive, vendor-locked solutions in real-world venues. It also invites evaluation of trade-offs around reliability, maintenance, and support in commercial deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位工程师记录用约 1600 美元的 ESP32 方案替代原本价值约 12 万美元的保龄球馆控制系统。此项目展示了商用场景中，普通微控制器与开源软件仍有能力替代昂贵的、厂商绑定的解决方案。亦需权衡可靠性、维护与售后支持等现实挑战。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(3) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/hugging-face-model-evaluation-security-incident/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI and Hugging Face address security incident during model evaluation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 与 Hugging Face 解决模型评估中的安全事件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48997548' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1590</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48997548' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1110</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI and Hugging Face coordinated after a security incident affecting model evaluation across their platforms. They outline the investigation steps, remediation actions, and ongoing safeguards to prevent data leaks and misuse during evaluation. The episode underscores the fragility of cross-platform evaluation pipelines and the need for hardened security controls in AI collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 与 Hugging Face 在一次影响模型评估的安全事件后开展协作，披露调查进展、整改措施及持续的安全防护。此次事件凸显跨平台评估流程的脆弱性，以及在 AI 领域加强安全控制的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/1kXZRSOAQbv2BDKEO14tJl/b2f0a336afaddc32e5776ba555bc4dbf/Hugging_Face_Security_Incident_16x9.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' data-full='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/1kXZRSOAQbv2BDKEO14tJl/b2f0a336afaddc32e5776ba555bc4dbf/Hugging_Face_Security_Incident_16x9.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49022152' data-tags='Education,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(4) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nealstephenson.substack.com/p/writing-by-hand-is-good-for-your' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Writing by hand is good for your brain</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>手写对大脑有益</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49022152' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1455</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49022152' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 655</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Handwriting engages motor memory and deeper processing, often boosting recall more than typing for learning tasks. The act of forming letters seems to strengthen neural connections that support long-term memory and understanding. For students and knowledge workers, mixing handwritten notes with digital tools can improve retention.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>手写能激活运动记忆并促使更深入的信息处理，常常比打字在学习任务中带来更高的记忆保留。手写过程有助于强化支持长期记忆和理解的神经连接。对于学生和知识工作者，混合使用手写笔记与数字工具有利于提升记忆留存。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48979269' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(5) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://werd.io/american-ai-is-locked-down-and-proprietary-its-losing/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>China’s open-weights AI strategy is winning</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>中国的开放权重AI策略正在取得胜利</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48979269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1226</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48979269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 925</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>China is pushing an open-weights AI strategy—favoring open-source models, shared datasets, and domestic collaboration. This approach is gaining momentum and challenging the dominance of proprietary AI, potentially accelerating adoption across sectors and reshaping global AI competition.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>中国正在推动开放权重的 AI 策略，重点支持开源模型、共用数据集与国内生态协作。这一路径正在获得势头，挑战以往的专有 AI 主导地位，或将加速各行业的应用并改变全球 AI 竞争格局。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1548192746-dd526f154ed9?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3wxMTc3M3wwfDF8c2VhcmNofDJ8fGluZGllfGVufDB8fHx8MTc0OTY5ODcxM3ww&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=2000' data-full='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1548192746-dd526f154ed9?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3wxMTc3M3wwfDF8c2VhcmNofDJ8fGluZGllfGVufDB8fHx8MTc0OTY5ODcxM3ww&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=2000' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(6) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chatgpt.com/share/6a5fdc7a-d6f8-83e8-bbea-8deb42cfed56' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Terence Tao&#x27;s ChatGPT conversation about the Jacobian Conjecture counterexample</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>陶哲軒與 ChatGPT 的 Jacobian 猜想反例對話</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49010345' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1105</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49010345' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 628</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Terrence Tao engages ChatGPT in a discussion about the Jacobian Conjecture, exploring a potential counterexample and the AI&#x27;s reasoning limits. The exchange highlights both the promise and the pitfalls of using language models for advanced mathematical problem-solving.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>宇宙数学大师泰罗（Terrence Tao）与 ChatGPT 展开对话，探讨雅可比猜想的潜在反例及 AI 推理的局限性。此互动凸显了将大型语言模型用于高层次数学推理的前景与风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.openai.com/chatgpt/share-og.png' data-full='https://cdn.openai.com/chatgpt/share-og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(7) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ads.openai.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Advertise in ChatGPT</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在ChatGPT上投放广告</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48996571' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1071</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48996571' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 825</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI opens a new advertising option within ChatGPT, letting brands reach engaged users in chat sessions. This monetization move could reshape user experience and ad-tech expectations as conversational interfaces become a media channel.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 在 ChatGPT 内开放广告位，让品牌在对话中触达用户。这一举措将影响用户体验与广告生态，改变以对话为媒介的变现与投放策略。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(8) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.politico.com/news/2026/07/22/startup-founders-urge-trump-not-to-shut-off-chinese-open-weight-ai-01008992' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Startup founders urge U.S. government not to shut off Chinese open weight AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>创业者敦促特朗普不要切断对中国开源权重 AI 的访问</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49023016' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1052</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49023016' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 868</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Founders argue that cutting off access to Chinese open-weight AI would throttle innovation, raise costs, and fragment global collaboration. They call for governance and standards instead of broad bans, highlighting that open weights accelerate safety research and tooling when responsibly managed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>创始人认为切断中国开源权重 AI 的访问会扼杀创新、提高成本，并分裂全球协作。他们呼吁以治理和标准取代全面禁令，强调在负责任管理下，开源权重能加速安全研究和工具开发。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(9) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bento.page/slides/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Bento - An entire PowerPoint in one HTML file (edit+view+data+collab)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Bento——把整份 PowerPoint 装进一个 HTML 文件（可编辑、查看、数据与协作）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49008211' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 999</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49008211' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 234</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bento packaging turns an entire PowerPoint into a single HTML file, with built-in editing, viewing, data integration, and collaboration. This approach enables portable, offline-friendly presentations and simplifies sharing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bento 将整份 PowerPoint 打包进一个 HTML 文件，具备编辑、查看、数据整合与协作功能。实现了演示文稿的便携、离线友好与简化分享。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49045159' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(10) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kitsumed.github.io/blog/posts/android-may-soon-restrict-on-device-adb/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Android may soon restrict on-device ADB</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Android 可能很快限制设备端 ADB</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49045159' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 955</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49045159' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 470</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google is tightening on-device ADB controls, requiring user authorization for new hosts and limiting access to trusted contexts. The move aims to shrink the attack surface of debugging tools on Android, but may complicate automation and enterprise workflows. Expect gradual rollout across upcoming Android updates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Android 正在加强对设备端 ADB 的控制，要求对新主机进行授权并限制对受信任环境以外的访问。此举旨在降低调试工具的攻击面，但可能增加自动化与企业工作流的难度。相关改动将会在未来的 Android 更新中逐步落地。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://kitsumed.goatcounter.com/count?p=/blog/posts/android-may-soon-restrict-on-device-adb&amp;t=Android%20May%20Soon%20Restrict%20On-Device%20ADB%2c%20Affecting%20Shizuku%2c%20libadb%20and%20Developers' data-full='https://kitsumed.goatcounter.com/count?p=/blog/posts/android-may-soon-restrict-on-device-adb&amp;t=Android%20May%20Soon%20Restrict%20On-Device%20ADB%2c%20Affecting%20Shizuku%2c%20libadb%20and%20Developers' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49012070' data-tags='Culture,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(11) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/na_announce/status/2079952538040672302' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>John C. Dvorak has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>约翰·C·德沃拉克去世</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49012070' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 920</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49012070' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 311</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Veteran tech journalist John C. Dvorak has died, leaving a controversial but influential footprint in tech media and early PC culture. His outspoken opinions and long-running columns helped shape debates around computing hardware and industry trends.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>资深科技记者约翰·C·德沃拉克去世，他在科技媒体与早期个人电脑文化中留下了富有争议但具影响力的足迹。他直言不讳的观点与长篇专栏塑造了关于计算硬件与行业趋势的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1350980458/na_twitter2_200x200.gif' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1350980458/na_twitter2_200x200.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://killthecookiebanner.eu/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kill The Cookie Banner</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>抵制 Cookie 横幅计划</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49057175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 899</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49057175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 439</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A privacy-focused initiative that advocates simpler consent UX and fewer cookie banners, potentially proposing standards or tools to reduce intrusive tracking prompts. This movement aims to improve browsing experience while preserving essential privacy controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该计划推动更简洁的同意界面与更少的 Cookie 横幅，提出降低追踪提示的标准或工具，改善浏览体验的同时保持隐私保护。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://killthecookiebanner.eu/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/kill-the-cookie-banner-logo.png' data-full='https://killthecookiebanner.eu/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/kill-the-cookie-banner-logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(13) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/Alibaba_Qwen/status/2078759124914098291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qwen 3.8</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Qwen 3.8 版本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48966120' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 878</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48966120' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 604</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Alibaba&#x27;s Qwen 3.8 is the latest release in its Qwen family, aiming to improve instruction-following and response quality. The update underscores a continued push by Chinese tech firms to scale capable LLMs for both consumer apps and enterprise use. As with similar releases, safety, guardrails, and domain-specific tuning are likely key focus areas.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阿里巴巴推出 Qwen 3.8，作为 Qwen 系列的最新版本，力求提升指令遵循能力与输出质量。此举反映中国厂商在大模型领域继续扩张的趋势，覆盖消费者应用与企业场景。与其他厂商的更新一样，安全性、约束机制和领域定制将成为核心关注点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HNk8XP_aoAAUShj.jpg:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HNk8XP_aoAAUShj.jpg:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(14) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fireworks.ai/blog/kimik3-fable' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kimi K3 Is Competitive with Fable; Kimi K3 and Fable Is SoTA</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kimi K3 与 Fable 比肩，达到顶尖水平</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48999291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 863</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48999291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 434</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kimi K3 delivers competitive performance against Fable, suggesting it has reached a state-of-the-art level in certain benchmarks. The claim underscores a rising parity among high-end open/white-box models, intensifying competition in the LLM space.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kimi K3 的表现与 Fable 相当，在某些基准测试中已接近顶尖水平。此举反映出高端开源模型之间日益接近的实力，对 LLM 生态的竞争格局将产生重要影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/pv37i0yn/production/4f0b611f9b0ec4e15ea45aabe369a3ba88fe4abf-2048x1152.svg' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/pv37i0yn/production/4f0b611f9b0ec4e15ea45aabe369a3ba88fe4abf-2048x1152.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(15) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stratechery.com/2026/whos-afraid-of-chinese-models/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Who&#x27;s afraid of Chinese models?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谁害怕中国模型？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48977128' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 853</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48977128' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 684</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stratechery argues that fear of Chinese AI models may be overstated and discusses geopolitical dynamics around model development, data, and competitiveness. It suggests Western firms should rethink dependency and collaboration strategies rather than succumb to alarm about Chinese tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Stratechery 指出对中国模型的恐惧或许被高估，分析了在模型开发、数据与竞争力方面的地缘政治动态。文章建议西方公司重新思考依赖与合作策略，而非对中国技术过度惊慌。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s0.wp.com/_si/?t=eyJpbWciOiJodHRwczpcL1wvaTAud3AuY29tXC9zdHJhdGVjaGVyeS5jb21cL3dwLWNvbnRlbnRcL3VwbG9hZHNcLzIwMThcLzAzXC9jcm9wcGVkLWFuZHJvaWQtY2hyb21lLTUxMng1MTItMS5wbmc_Zml0PTUxMiUyQzUxMiZzc2w9MSIsInR4dCI6IlN0cmF0ZWNoZXJ5IGJ5IEJlbiBUaG9tcHNvbiIsInRlbXBsYXRlIjoiZWRnZSIsImZvbnQiOiIiLCJibG9nX2lkIjoxODgwNDM0MTV9.5VWck4PcKPWCTPe_HVznn3n3xsgn-G0b3d2OeiNNC7cMQ' data-full='https://s0.wp.com/_si/?t=eyJpbWciOiJodHRwczpcL1wvaTAud3AuY29tXC9zdHJhdGVjaGVyeS5jb21cL3dwLWNvbnRlbnRcL3VwbG9hZHNcLzIwMThcLzAzXC9jcm9wcGVkLWFuZHJvaWQtY2hyb21lLTUxMng1MTItMS5wbmc_Zml0PTUxMiUyQzUxMiZzc2w9MSIsInR4dCI6IlN0cmF0ZWNoZXJ5IGJ5IEJlbiBUaG9tcHNvbiIsInRlbXBsYXRlIjoiZWRnZSIsImZvbnQiOiIiLCJibG9nX2lkIjoxODgwNDM0MTV9.5VWck4PcKPWCTPe_HVznn3n3xsgn-G0b3d2OeiNNC7cMQ' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A look at airport simulation software and its potential to improve training, operations planning, and passenger flow analysis.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文概览机场仿真软件及其在培训、运营规划和旅客流量分析方面的潜在作用。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49033004' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ptrchm.com/posts/nothing-works-and-everyone-is-euphoric/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If coding has been solved, why does software keep getting worse?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如果编码已被解决，软件为何还在变坏？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49033004' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 815</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49033004' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 617</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that while fundamentals of programming haven&#x27;t been solved, software quality keeps deteriorating due to incentives, complexity, and rushed delivery. The piece critiques hype and explores how teams manage complexity, proposing signals for healthier practices like modularity and testing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为编程基础并未真正被解决，但软件质量仍在恶化，原因在于激励、复杂度与匆促交付。批评行业热潮背后的错配，讨论如何通过模块化、测试等方式提升软件质量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ptrchm.com/images/photo.jpg' data-full='https://ptrchm.com/images/photo.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://xcancel.com/__alpoge__/status/2079028340955197566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Fable produced a counterexample to the Jacobian Conjecture</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Fable 提出雅可比猜想的反例</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48973869' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 772</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48973869' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 482</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A claimed counterexample to the Jacobian Conjecture circulated, challenging a long-standing open problem in algebraic geometry. Such claims are subjected to rigorous verification, as a verified counterexample would upend established theory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称出现了针对雅可比猜想的反例，令这一长期悬而未决的问题成为焦点。此类主张需要严格验证，一旦成立将彻底改变相关理论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://xcancel.com/logo.png' data-full='https://xcancel.com/logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/models-and-research/gemini-models/gemini-3-6-flash-3-5-flash-lite-3-5-flash-cyber/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gemini 3.6 Flash, 3.5 Flash-Lite, and 3.5 Flash Cyber</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gemini 3.6 Flash、3.5 Flash-Lite 与 3.5 Flash-Cyber</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48993414' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 746</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48993414' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 572</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google rolls out Gemini 3.6 alongside two lighter/specialized variants: 3.5 Flash-Lite and 3.5 Flash-Cyber. The Flash family targets faster, lighter inference for consumer apps and code-related tasks, expanding Gemini&#x27;s reach across devices and workloads. This tiered lineup signals Google&#x27;s continued push to optimize latency, cost, and safety across use cases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌推出 Gemini 3.6，并辅以两款轻量化/专业化的 Flash 版本：3.5 Flash-Lite 与 3.5 Flash-Cyber。Flash 系列强调在消费级应用和代码场景下的低延迟、低资源占用，拓展 Gemini 的适用场景。此举体现谷歌在用例分层、降低成本与提升安全性方面的持续布局。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/gemini-3-5_3-6_3-5-Cyber__key-art__statement_.width-1300.jpg' data-full='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/gemini-3-5_3-6_3-5-Cyber__key-art__statement_.width-1300.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49032660' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://glyphack.com/attention/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>It&#x27;s getting harder to focus every day</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>专注力每天都在下降</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49032660' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 744</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49032660' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 402</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Focus is slipping in an age of constant distractions—from notifications to algorithmic feeds. The piece suggests practical shifts like batching deep work, reducing context switching, and hardening personal workflows to reclaim attention.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在持续的通知和算法信息流冲击下，专注力日益下降。文中给出减少分心、将深度工作集中在固定时段，以及优化日常工作流等应对策略。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48963879' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://workshop.cjpais.com/projects/transcribe-cpp' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Transcribe.cpp</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Transcribe.cpp 实现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48963879' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 743</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48963879' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 156</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Transcribe.cpp presents a compact C++ project that demonstrates a speech-to-text workflow. It walks through building a lightweight transcription pipeline and discusses latency versus accuracy trade-offs, including how to structure streaming versus batch transcription.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Transcribe.cpp 展示了一个精简的 C++ 转写实现，展示了将音频转换为文本的基本流程，并讨论了延迟与准确率之间的权衡，包含流式与批量转写的取舍。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48978605' data-tags='Security,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(22) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.risky.biz/risky-bulletin-hacker-wipes-romanias-entire-land-registry-database/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hacker wipes Romania&#x27;s land registry database</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>黑客抹除罗马尼亚土地登记数据库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48978605' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 703</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48978605' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 402</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hacker reportedly wiped Romania&#x27;s entire land registry database, threatening the integrity of property records and disrupting transactions. The incident highlights catastrophic risks from insufficient backups, disaster recovery planning, and the high stakes of protecting centralized public registries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有黑客据称清除了罗马尼亚整个土地登记数据库，威胁到财产记录的完整性并干扰交易。此事凸显了集中式公共登记在备份、灾难恢复和安全保护不足时会带来的严重风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/16/e9/16e9a748-ca66-4b3c-8590-85537131f696/content/images/2026/07/000-RBN-logo-4.png' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/16/e9/16e9a748-ca66-4b3c-8590-85537131f696/content/images/2026/07/000-RBN-logo-4.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48996318' data-tags='Open Source,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://freeink.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FreeInk: Open ecosystem for e-readers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Freeink：开源电子书阅读器开放生态</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48996318' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 703</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48996318' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 157</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Freeink aims to create an open ecosystem for e-readers, enabling interoperability among devices, apps, and content. By reducing vendor lock-in, it could accelerate community-driven improvements and privacy options.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Freeink 旨在建立面向电子书阅读器的开放生态，促进设备、应用与内容之间的互操作性。降低厂商锁定可能推动社区驱动的改进，并提升隐私选项。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48997221' data-tags='Privacy,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(24) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techradar.com/vpn/vpn-privacy-security/vpns-are-lawful-technical-tools-says-eu-court-in-landmark-anne-frank-copyright-ruling' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#x27;VPNs are lawful technical tools,&#x27; says EU Court in landmark copyright ruling</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟法院裁定 VPN 为合法技术工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48997221' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 685</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48997221' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 139</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The EU Court ruled that VPNs are lawful technical tools with legitimate use cases in protecting privacy and enabling access to information. This ruling clarifies that circumvention or privacy protections are not inherently illegal, shaping how copyright enforcement and online freedom interplay. It signals a supportive stance for privacy-preserving tech in Europe.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟法院裁定 VPN 为合法的技术工具，强调其在保护隐私和访问信息方面的正当用途。该判决澄清了 VPN 使用本身并非非法，对提供商与用户均有积极影响，同时为欧洲的版权执法与互联网自由定调。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/5NsvMB6arEjzGkWxkstoDj-1920-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/5NsvMB6arEjzGkWxkstoDj-1920-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(25) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://futurism.com/artificial-intelligence/ai-companies-hide-debt-off-balance-sheet' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI Companies Are Trying to Hide a Staggering Amount of Debt</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 公司正隐藏巨额债务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49020999' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 683</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49020999' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 372</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI firms have reportedly buried large debt off their balance sheets, highlighting a riskier funding landscape as capital markets back AI bets. The disclosure questions the true financial health of high-flying AI startups and their burn rates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称 AI 公司在账面之外隐藏了巨额债务，暴露在对 AI 投资热潮中的资金风险。此类披露质疑这些高增长企业的真实财务状况和资金消耗速度。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(26) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dylancastillo.co/posts/pelicanmaxxing.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Are AI labs pelicanmaxxing?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 研究室到底是在追逐 Pelicanmaxxing 吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49010129' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 673</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49010129' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 240</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece questions whether AI labs optimize for sensational growth and hype over careful safety and long-term research, coining &#x27;pelicanmaxxing&#x27; as a metaphor. It argues for clearer incentives and more responsible development practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章质疑 AI 研究室是否以引人注目的扩张和炒作来取代稳健的安全与长期研究，并以“Pelicanmaxxing”作为隐喻。提出需要更清晰的激励与更负责任的开发方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dylancastillo.co/posts/images/social_media_card.webp' data-full='https://dylancastillo.co/posts/images/social_media_card.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(28) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/space/2026/07/indias-first-privately-developed-rocket-reaches-orbit-on-dramatic-debut-launch/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>India&#x27;s first privately-developed rocket reaches orbit on debut launch</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>印度首枚私营开发火箭首次进入轨道</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48973835' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 667</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48973835' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 206</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>India&#x27;s first privately-developed rocket reached orbit on its debut, signaling a new era for the country&#x27;s private space sector. The milestone underscores growing private capabilities, potential disruption to traditional launch dynamics, and global competition in access to space.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>印度首枚私营开发火箭在首秀中进入轨道，标志着印度私营航天进入新阶段。此成就显示私人能力的提升，或对传统发射格局带来影响，并反映全球在进入太空领域的竞争加剧。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OverpAId：解雇CEO，雇佣未来</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49004663' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 662</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49004663' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 347</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take on AI leadership that envisions firing human CEOs in favor of AI executives. It outlines potential benefits like faster decision cycles and lower costs, but also flags governance, accountability, and oversight risks in AI-driven organizations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个颠覆性观点，设想用 AI 取代人类首席执行官。论述了更快的决策周期和成本节省等潜在好处，同时指出在 AI 驱动的企业中治理、问责和监督等风险。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(30) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jelly-ui.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jelly UI: Soft-body physics for native HTML form controls</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Jelly UI：原生 HTML 表单控件的软体物理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48981620' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 656</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48981620' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 201</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jelly UI experiments with soft-body physics applied to native HTML form controls, delivering more tactile interactions without heavy dependencies. If adopted, it could influence micro-interactions across web forms and spark new design ideas.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Jelly UI 将软体物理应用到原生 HTML 表单控件，带来更具触感的交互，且无需引入较重的依赖。若普及，可能影响表单的微交互设计并激发新的设计灵感。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49035303' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(31) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnbc.com/2026/07/24/nvidia-microsoft-meta-open-weight-ai-models.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nvidia, Microsoft, Meta warn against overregulating open-weight models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英伟达、微软、Meta 警告勿过度监管开放权重模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49035303' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 650</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49035303' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 313</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NVIDIA, Microsoft, and Meta warn against overregulating open-weight models, arguing that heavy-handed rules could hamper innovation and push developers to closed ecosystems. They advocate targeted, risk-based oversight rather than broad bans, stressing practical governance over blanket prohibitions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英伟达、微软、Meta 警告不要对开放权重模型进行过度监管，担心会抑制创新并推动开发者转向封闭生态系统。他们主张以风险为基础的、有针对性的监管，而非全面禁令，强调治理应兼顾现实世界的应用场景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.cnbcfm.com/api/v1/image/108173882-1752844539526-gettyimages-2225696381-mms28707_1cm2uml7.jpeg?v=1752844582&amp;w=1920&amp;h=1080' data-full='https://image.cnbcfm.com/api/v1/image/108173882-1752844539526-gettyimages-2225696381-mms28707_1cm2uml7.jpeg?v=1752844582&amp;w=1920&amp;h=1080' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49010648' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 631</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49010648' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 247</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张开发者应掌握 SIMD，以利用 CPU 的矢量化性能，并给出跨语言的实践要点与示例。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49034292' data-tags='Security,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(33) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hhh.hn/hanwha-github-token/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>My security camera shipped a GitHub admin token in its login page</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我的安防摄像头在登录页暴露了一个 GitHub 管理员令牌</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49034292' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 621</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49034292' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 205</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A security camera shipped with a GitHub admin token embedded in its login page, potentially exposing credentials to anyone who visits. This incident highlights IoT authentication risks, insecure UI design, and the importance of secure firmware updates and token rotation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安防摄像头的登录页暴露了一个 GitHub 管理员令牌，可能被任意访问者利用。这一事件凸显物联网认证风险、界面安全设计不足，以及对固件更新和令牌轮换的迫切需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/e/e7/Dynamic_Front_25_-_Finnish_Army_K9_Moukari_Self-Propelled_Howitzer_%288761382%29.jpg/1280px-Dynamic_Front_25_-_Finnish_Army_K9_Moukari_Self-Propelled_Howitzer_%288761382%29.jpg' data-full='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/e/e7/Dynamic_Front_25_-_Finnish_Army_K9_Moukari_Self-Propelled_Howitzer_%288761382%29.jpg/1280px-Dynamic_Front_25_-_Finnish_Army_K9_Moukari_Self-Propelled_Howitzer_%288761382%29.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/marcelroed/gigatoken/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GigaToken: ~1000x faster Language model tokenization</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GigaToken：语言模型分词速度提升约 1000 倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49010167' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 608</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49010167' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 119</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GigaToken claims tokenization speedups around 1000x for language models, potentially lowering preprocessing bottlenecks for large-scale deployments. If practical, it could shift cost and performance considerations for AI products that rely on tokenization as a bottleneck.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GigaToken 声称能将语言模型分词速度提升约 1000×，显著降低大规模部署的预处理瓶颈。若落地可行，将改变依赖分词的 AI 产品的成本与性能权衡。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.maths.cam.ac.uk/features/professor-hannah-fry-wins-leelavati-prize' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hannah Fry Wins the Leelavati Prize in 2026 for Mathematics Outreach</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49043724' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 581</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49043724' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 109</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hannah Fry is awarded the Leelavati Prize for mathematics outreach, recognizing her work turning complex math ideas into accessible public-facing content. The award underscores the value of science communication in expanding participation in STEM and inspires broader engagement with mathematical ideas.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>汉娜·弗莱因其将复杂数学观念转化为易于大众理解的内容而获颁 Leelavati 数学传播奖，肯定了她在科学传播方面的贡献。该奖项强调了公众沟通在扩大 STEM 参与度方面的重要性，并激励更多人关注与学习数学。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49007374' data-tags='Security,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(37) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/nikitabier/status/2079787406300266743' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Passkeys were invented by engineers with zero understanding of consumer brain</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Passkeys 由对用户认知不足的工程师发明</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49007374' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 560</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49007374' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 778</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Passkeys aim to replace passwords with cryptographic credentials, but critics argue the UX reflects engineers’ blind spots about how real users think. Adoption friction could hobble the technology unless UX and recovery flows are rethought.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Passkeys 被视为替代密码的安全升级，但有人批评其用户体验暴露了工程师对普通用户认知的盲点。若不改进 UX 与恢复流程，普及应用将面临更大阻力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1755448801957945344/Fh2HNw5Y_200x200.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1755448801957945344/Fh2HNw5Y_200x200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Qwen-Image-3.0 showcases richer content, authentic details, and deeper knowledge in its multimodal capabilities. The update promises improved image generation with factual grounding and better contextual reasoning, appealing to designers, educators, and analysts. This signals a step toward more trustworthy AI-assisted visual content.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Qwen-Image-3.0 展现出更丰富的内容、更真实的细节，以及更深的知识储备，提升了多模态能力。更新强调在生成图像时的事实性与上下文推理，适用于设计师、教育工作者等对视觉内容可靠性有高要求的场景。这标志着 AI 辅助视觉内容可信度的提升。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Reddit 竟然认定纯 HTML 不再安全</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49005747' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 558</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49005747' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 573</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes Reddit’s move to treat plain HTML as unsafe content, aiming to curb misuse and scripting abuse. The change affects developers relying on HTML embeds and has implications for moderation and third-party tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>描述 Reddit 将纯 HTML 视为不安全内容的举措，意在遏制滥用与脚本滥用。这一变动影响依赖 HTML 嵌入的开发者，并对治理和第三方工具带来影响。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis arguing that OpenAI&#x27;s accidental cyber attack on Hugging Face mirrors a sci-fi security scenario playing out in the real world, highlighting cross-platform security risks.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jdan.github.io/98.css/#status-bar' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>98.css</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>98.css：让网页重现 Windows 98 风格的 CSS 框架</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49028927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 543</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49028927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 124</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>98.css is a tiny CSS framework that recreates Windows 98-era UI with modern HTML. It enables quick retro-style demos and nostalgia-driven UI experiments without heavy dependencies. The project showcases how small CSS kits can deliver a complete desktop-like aesthetic with just a few lines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>98.css 是一个小型 CSS 框架，能够把网页渲染成 Windows 98 时代的界面。它便于快速做出复古风格的仪表板或演示，而无需繁重的依赖。该项目展示了极简 CSS 组件也能实现桌面级美观的效果。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49038060' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/jul/24/openai-rogue-hacker' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Be skeptical of OpenAI&#x27;s rogue hacker agent story</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对 OpenAI 的 rogue hacker agent 故事保持怀疑</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49038060' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 527</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49038060' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 294</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Questioning the veracity of a reported OpenAI rogue hacker agent incident, the piece argues for skepticism and better verification in AI security reporting. It discusses how sensational stories can mislead about AI autonomy and security risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章对关于 OpenAI 失控黑客代理的报道保持怀疑，强调需要更严格的核验与背景信息。讨论此类新闻对 AI 安全感知的影响以及报道方式的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/e3c91fad3c0af25a6cbbd44c6b091bdfdfdd270d/952_0_4448_3558/master/4448.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctb3BpbmlvbnMucG5n&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=d8a12eef6083a120c19a454c8d40fcfd' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/e3c91fad3c0af25a6cbbd44c6b091bdfdfdd270d/952_0_4448_3558/master/4448.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctb3BpbmlvbnMucG5n&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=d8a12eef6083a120c19a454c8d40fcfd' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(45) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thehindu.com/news/national/government-orders-github-to-remove-bluetooth-based-chat-app-bitchat-over-security-concerns-jack-dorsey/article71262049.ece' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Government orders GitHub to remove Bluetooth-based chat app Bitchat: Jack Dorsey</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>政府要求 GitHub 下架蓝牙聊天应用 Bitchat，涉及安全担忧</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49036433' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 526</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49036433' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 431</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A government directive orders removal of a Bluetooth-based chat app linked to a high-profile figure, citing security concerns. The case highlights how policy actions intersect with platform hosting and privacy in distributed apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>政府指令要求下架与高知名人物相关的蓝牙聊天应用，因安全担忧而采取行动。此事凸显政策行为与平台托管、隐私在分布式应用场景中的交叉影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(46) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://edri.org/our-work/the-eu-is-about-to-sell-our-most-sensitive-data-to-the-us-for-visa-free-travel/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The EU is about to sell our most sensitive data to the US for visa-free travel</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟将把最敏感数据共享给美国以实现免签旅行</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48977711' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 521</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48977711' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 318</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>European authorities are moving toward sharing sensitive traveler data with the United States to enable visa-free travel. The plan raises privacy, data protection, and oversight concerns about transatlantic data flows and potential surveillance risks. It highlights tensions between security goals and GDPR-era protections, and may push regulators to tighten controls on data access and purpose limitation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟拟向美国共享敏感的旅行者数据以实现免签措施，这引发对隐私、数据保护和跨境数据流的担忧。相关安排可能加剧对监控与数据使用边界的质疑，并促使监管机构就数据访问权限和用途限制加强把关。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://edri.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/2026_1Artboard-7.png' data-full='https://edri.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/2026_1Artboard-7.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(47) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/2026/Jul/19/claude-code-in-bun-in-rust/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Code uses Bun written in Rust now</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Code 现已在 Bun 上运行，核心以 Rust 编写</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48966569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 512</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48966569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 678</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code has been reworked to run on Bun, with the runtime implemented in Rust. The change promises faster startup and reduced overhead, potentially broadening compatibility and enabling smoother integration with Rust-native tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code 已改为在 Bun 运行，运行时以 Rust 实现。这一改动有望提升启动速度和性能开销，便于与 Rust 原生工具链的集成。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(50) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chipweinberger.com/articles/20260719-hardware-is-not-so-hard' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What I learned selling 2,500 MIDI recorders: Hardware is not so hard</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>卖出2500个 MIDI 记录器让我明白硬件并不难</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48966713' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 487</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48966713' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 221</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author shares hands-on lessons from selling 2,500 MIDI recorders, arguing that hardware development is more approachable than it seems with smart sourcing, tooling, and customer feedback loops. Expect practical notes on prototyping, cost control, manufacturing, and post-sale support for indie hardware projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者在销售2500个 MIDI 记录器的过程中总结了实战经验，认为在正确的 sourcing、工具链和客户反馈循环下，硬件开发并不如想象中那么难。文章提供从原型、成本控制到制造与售后支持的实用要点，适合独立硬件创业者借鉴。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://chipweinberger.com/assets/20260719-hardware-is-not-so-hard/jamcorder-in-situ.png' data-full='https://chipweinberger.com/assets/20260719-hardware-is-not-so-hard/jamcorder-in-situ.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48983382' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(51) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://xenaproject.wordpress.com/2026/07/20/human-mathematicians-are-being-outcounterexampled/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Human mathematicians are being outcounterexampled</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人类数学家正被反例发现所超越</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48983382' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 486</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48983382' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 249</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI systems are increasingly able to produce counterexamples to mathematical conjectures, sometimes outperforming humans at this specific task. This shift reshapes collaboration between mathematicians and machines, emphasizing verification, reproducibility, and new roles for intuition in research. The trend could accelerate discovery but also demands careful framing of proofs and trust in automated reasoning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>人工智能系统日益擅长发现数学猜想的反例，有时在这个特定任务上甚至优于人类。这种变化正在重新定义数学家与机器之间的协作，强调验证、可重复性，以及直觉在研究中的新角色。此趋势可能加速发现，但也需要对证明与自动推理的信任进行审慎权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://xenaproject.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/05/cropped-twitchpfc.png?w=200' data-full='https://xenaproject.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/05/cropped-twitchpfc.png?w=200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49007247' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(52) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://resobscura.substack.com/p/quality-non-fiction-books-are-the' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Quality non-fiction books are the antithesis of AI slop</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>高质量非虚构书籍是 AI 俗作文的对立面</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49007247' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 483</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49007247' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 235</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A pointed critique arguing that well-researched, carefully written non-fiction remains essential in a world chasing AI-generated content. The piece emphasizes rigor, citations, and nuance as defining traits that distinguish quality writing from AI churn.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇尖锐的评论认为经过充分研究、精心撰写的非虚构作品在追逐 AI 内容的时代依然至关重要。作者强调严格性、引文与语义深度，指出这些是区分高质量写作与 AI 产出差异的关键。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49028037' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(53) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49028037' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tell HN: Namecheap gave my account to an unverified third party</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tell HN：Namecheap 将我的账户交给未验证的第三方</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49028037' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 476</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49028037' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 174</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A user says Namecheap handed control of their account to an unverified third party, exposing domain settings and billing to risk. This highlights gaps in identity verification and the need for stronger access controls and audit trails at registrars.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有用户称 Namecheap 将其账户的控制权交给未经验证的第三方，暴露域名设置与计费信息的风险。事件暴露了注册商身份验证的薄弱，以及需要更强的访问控制和审计跟踪。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48986731' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(54) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.aclu.org/news/privacy-technology/tracking-alpr-cameras/flock-safety-credibility-lost-as-it-repeatedly-lies-to-city-councils-police-departments-and-public-across-the-country' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Flock Credibility Lost as It Repeatedly Lies to City Councils, Police, &amp; Public</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Flock 公信力崩塌：多次向市议会、警察与公众撒谎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48986731' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 463</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48986731' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 163</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An ACLU report accuses Flock Safety of repeatedly misrepresenting facts about ALPR cameras to city councils, police, and the public. The allegations threaten regulatory scrutiny and trust in automated enforcement tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ACLU 的报告指控 Flock Safety 多次对市议会、警方和公众歪曲关于自动车牌识别系统的事实。这些指控可能引发监管审查并动摇对自动执法技术的信任。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assets.aclu.org/live/uploads/2026/07/flock-camera-closeup-s.jpg' data-full='https://assets.aclu.org/live/uploads/2026/07/flock-camera-closeup-s.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49026810' data-tags='AI,Show HN'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49026810' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Echo – Fable-level results at 1/3 the cost using open-weight models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Echo——用开放权重模型以三分之一成本获得同级别结果</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49026810' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 460</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49026810' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 218</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Echo demonstrates top-tier results at about one-third the cost by using open-weight models. This highlights the viability of open-weight AI for cost-sensitive deployments, though tradeoffs in support and ecosystem maturity remain.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Echo 在开放权重模型的帮助下，以约三分之一的成本取得接近顶级基准的结果，显示开源权重在降低 AI 部署成本方面的潜力。与此同时，生态成熟度和技术支持等取舍需要考虑。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hatchet.run/blog/postgres-survival-guide' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The startup&#x27;s Postgres survival guide</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>初创公司的 PostgreSQL 生存指南</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49005787' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 459</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49005787' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 204</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PostgreSQL is a common choice for startups, but early ops teams juggle reliability, scale, and cost on tight budgets. This guide cuts through the noise with pragmatic tips on deployment, backups, monitoring, and when to opt for managed or serverless options to avoid ops debt. The core message: build conservatively, measure, and scale with minimal friction.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PostgreSQL 常被初创公司采用，但早期运维团队需在可靠性、扩展性与成本之间取舍。该指南提供务实做法，涵盖部署、备份、监控，以及何时选择托管或服务器无关的选项，以避免运维负担迅速攀升。核心要点是以简单、可度量、可扩展的方式稳步成长。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://citizendot.github.io/articles/fake-job-interview-git-hook-malware/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I Inspected My Take-Home Interview Project. It Was a Whole Operation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我审阅了我的 Take-Home 面试项目，它竟然是一整套操作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49013036' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 457</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49013036' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 124</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A look into a take-home interview project that turned out to be a larger operational affair, including a malware-laden Git hook. The piece underscores security risks in hiring processes and offers guidance for auditing take-home assignments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对一个 Take-Home 面试项目的审阅揭示其竟是一整套操作，甚至包含带有恶意 Git 钩子的实现。此文强调招聘流程中的安全风险，并为审查此类任务提供了实用建议。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techspot.com/news/113236-us-prosecutors-charge-atlanta-man-after-grapheneos-phone.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US citizen charged after GrapheneOS phone wipes during airport search</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国公民在机场安检时因 GrapheneOS 自动清除数据而被起诉</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49063022' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 455</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49063022' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 290</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The case highlights how privacy-focused devices like GrapheneOS can trigger data wipe during border checks, leading to legal action. It underscores tensions between personal data protection, law enforcement, and the risk of unintended data loss.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一起案例显示隐私导向设备在机场安检时可能因安全特性触发数据擦除，进而引发起诉。凸显了个人数据保护、执法权力与意外数据丢失之间的张力。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(59) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.ericgoldman.org/archives/2026/07/apple-defeats-liability-for-not-scanning-icloud-for-csam-but-the-judge-was-not-pleased-amy-v-apple.htm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple defeats liability for not scanning iCloud for CSAM</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果避免就未对 iCloud 进行 CSAM 扫描承担赔偿责任</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48992870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 448</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48992870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 539</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The court dismissed liability claims against Apple for not scanning iCloud for CSAM, marking a win for the company in a high-profile privacy-and-security case. The decision narrows the scope of potential liability for platform providers, though the judge expressed displeasure with the outcome, suggesting an appetite for appeal or legislative scrutiny.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法院对苹果因未对 iCloud 内容执行 CSAM 扫描的指控认定无责，为公司在隐私与安全责任纠纷中取得胜利。这一裁定缩小了对平台有义务主动扫描用户数据的潜在诉求，但主审法官对结果表示不满，暗示未来可能上诉或引发立法关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.ericgoldman.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/IMG_2022-1024x1020.jpg' data-full='https://blog.ericgoldman.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/IMG_2022-1024x1020.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://krebsonsecurity.com/2026/07/lg-to-ban-residential-proxies-from-smart-tv-apps/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LG to ban residential proxies from smart TV apps</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LG 将禁用智能电视应用中的住宅代理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49000864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 445</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49000864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 461</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LG plans to block residential proxies from apps on smart TVs, citing misuse such as content access abuse, fraud, and licensing circumvention. The move tightens control over how devices access services and may affect legitimate testing or regional experimentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LG 将在智能电视应用中禁止使用住宅代理，意在遏制滥用、欺诈和绕过地区版权等问题。这一举措加强了设备对服务的访问控制，可能影响需要代理的正当测试与区域实验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://krebsonsecurity.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/pac-man-brightdata.png' data-full='https://krebsonsecurity.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/pac-man-brightdata.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这一项目聚焦数字沟通中的“被甩”现象，汇集相关案例与观察，解析人为何突然消失以及应如何应对。内容覆盖约会应用、即时通讯和社交平台的规律，为在现代沟通中遭遇模糊局面的人提供实用建议。</p>
