<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.mozilla.org/en/firefox/firefox-containers-preview/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Firefox Containers Preview</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Firefox 容器功能预览</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48995409' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 423</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48995409' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 118</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla previews Firefox Containers, a feature that isolates you into separate profiles for each site or context, improving privacy and reducing tracking across contexts. The preview covers UX, security trade-offs, and integration with existing Firefox features. This matters for users juggling multiple personas or sensitive data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla 公布了 Firefox 容器的预览版本，允许将不同站点或场景分隔到独立的容器中，以提升隐私与跨场景的跟踪防护。预览介绍了用户体验、安全权衡以及与现有功能的整合，对处理多种身份或敏感数据的用户尤为相关。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://claude.com/blog/the-new-rules-of-context-engineering-for-claude-5-generation-models' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The new rules of context engineering for Claude 5 generation models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude 5 代模型的上下文工程新规则</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic outlines new context engineering guidelines for Claude 5 generation models, detailing how to structure prompts, manage context windows, and orchestrate tool use to improve reliability and control. The rules reflect a shift toward modular, auditable prompts and better steerability in long-running interactions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 为 Claude 5 代模型制定了新的上下文工程规范，聚焦如何组织提示、管理上下文长度以及协调工具调用以提高稳定性与可控性。此举标志着在长期对话中对可预测性和审计性的重视。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(68) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://discuss.grapheneos.org/d/40700-grapheneos-protections-against-data-extraction-from-locked-devices' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GrapheneOS protections against data extraction from locked devices</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GrapheneOS 对锁定设备数据提取的保护机制</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49055169' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 400</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49055169' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 224</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS explains how its security model minimizes data leakage from locked devices, using robust encryption, strict isolation, and anti-forensic measures. The post clarifies what data remains inaccessible when the device is locked and what this means for user privacy and enterprise security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS 解释了在设备锁定时如何降低数据被提取的风险，依托强加密、严格隔离和防取证机制。文中说明锁屏状态下哪些数据不可访问，以及这对用户隐私和企业安全意味着什么。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(69) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://slcyber.io/research-center/exploit-brokers-pay-500000-for-a-wordpress-rce-i-found-one-with-gpt5-6/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I found a WordPress RCEs with GPT5.6 and $25</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我发现了一个用 GPT-5.6 的 WordPress 远程代码执行漏洞，且报价仅 $25</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48975665' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 398</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48975665' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 215</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A researcher exposes the underground market for WordPress RCEs, noting brokers pay six-figure sums for a single bug. They claim to have found a GPT-assisted WordPress RCE, illustrating how AI tools are used to craft exploits and why payouts can vary widely.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者揭示 WordPress 远程代码执行漏洞在地下市场的高额交易，某个漏洞的成交价可达到数十万美元。作者声称发现了一个由 GPT 辅助的 RCE 漏洞，展示了 AI 工具在漏洞利用中的应用以及出价的高度不确定性。文章揭示了 exploit 市场的盈利逻辑，以及为何 WordPress 仍然是高价值目标。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://slcyber.io/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Searchlight-Security-Research_Social_Dark_General.webp' data-full='https://slcyber.io/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Searchlight-Security-Research_Social_Dark_General.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49057241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 395</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49057241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 138</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Htmx 4.0 is released exclusively on Game Boy, a playful demonstration of bringing modern lightweight web UI concepts to retro hardware. The stunt sparks ideas about portability, WASM, and extreme constraint-driven design.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://wren.wtf/shower-thoughts/stop-using-opencode/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Annoying and alarming things about OpenCode</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48978112' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 394</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48978112' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 251</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Several annoyances and alarming issues are raised about OpenCode, including security concerns and governance questions. The piece urges readers to stop using OpenCode, arguing its design and policies introduce avoidable risks for developers and users. It serves as a cautionary note about evaluating tooling beyond hype.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中提出 OpenCode 存在的若干恼人及令人担忧的问题，包括安全性和治理方面的隐忧。作者呼吁停止使用该平台，认为其设计与政策会给开发者和用户带来不必要的风险。此文提醒人们在评价工具时要超越热度与宣传。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48993816' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://e360.yale.edu/digest/benin-coral-reef' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Long presumed dead, a thriving coral reef is discovered in West Africa</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>西非发现长期被误以为死亡的珊瑚礁仍然繁盛</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48993816' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 389</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48993816' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 92</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A coral reef off West Africa, long presumed dead, is thriving, challenging assumptions about reef decline in warming oceans. The discovery highlights resilience and underscores the importance of protecting regional marine habitats that sustain biodiversity and local communities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>西非沿岸一处长期被误以为已死的珊瑚礁却在繁盛生长，挑战了全球珊瑚礁衰退的普遍认知。此发现强调了区域海洋栖息地的韧性，并凸显保护对生物多样性与当地社区的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://yale-threesixty.transforms.svdcdn.com/production/Benin-Coral_IRHOB.jpg?w=1200&amp;q=60&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;dm=1784630734&amp;s=5aab36ee725d9087d4ce6272efc6d3ec' data-full='https://yale-threesixty.transforms.svdcdn.com/production/Benin-Coral_IRHOB.jpg?w=1200&amp;q=60&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;dm=1784630734&amp;s=5aab36ee725d9087d4ce6272efc6d3ec' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49048034' data-tags='AI,Open Source,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tobi.knaup.me/2026-07-25-open-weight-ai-is-having-its-kubernetes-moment/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Open-weight AI is having its Kubernetes moment</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开放权重 AI 正在迎来 Kubernetes 时刻</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49048034' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 388</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49048034' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 307</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Open-weight AI decouples model weights from compute and is finally seeing Kubernetes-native deployment tooling mature enough for production. This trend lets teams run, scale, and experiment with large models across clusters without tying weights to a single vendor. The shift accelerates collaboration and reproducibility in ML ops.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开放权重AI将模型权重与计算资源解耦，现阶段在云原生部署工具链上逐步成熟，利于企业落地。借助Kubernetes生态进行服务、监控与扩展，能够跨集群运行大模型，降低供应商锁定。该趋势将推动ML运维的协作性与可重复性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://psychotechnology.substack.com/p/em-dashes-are-fucking-amazing' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Em dashes are amazing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>破折号真的很棒</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49034747' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 382</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49034747' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 313</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>短评式文章赞美破折号在排版中的作用，讨论其可读性与风格贡献，并给出使用场景。</p>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49047453' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://refp.se/articles/your-shell-and-the-magic-colon' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A shell colon does nothing. Use it anyway</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Shell 的冒号啥也不干，但仍然有用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49047453' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 377</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49047453' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 157</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Colon in POSIX shells is a no-op builtin that always returns a success status. It’s surprisingly handy as a placeholder in complex one-liners, a guard in conditionals, and a vehicle for parameter-expansion tricks that don’t run any commands. The takeaway is: treat &#x27;:&#x27; as a lightweight, portable utility for robust, minimal bash scripting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 POSIX shell 中，冒号是一个无操作的内建命令，总是返回成功状态。它在复杂一行命令中的占位符、条件语句中的保护分支，以及无执行成本的参数扩展等场景中非常有用。要点是把 &#x27;:&#x27; 当作轻量、可移植的工具，用来写更健壮、简洁的脚本。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://refp.se/logo.png' data-full='https://refp.se/logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49023019' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/humanlayer/advanced-context-engineering-for-coding-agents/blob/main/wsff.md' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why Software Factories Fail (or: harness engineering is not enough)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么软件工厂会失败（或：仅靠工程化不足以成事）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49023019' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 377</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49023019' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 264</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that software factories fail not because of tech per se but due to overreliance on harness engineering and process alone. The piece suggests you need broader context engineering, governance, and guardrails to build reliable AI-enabled software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文指出，仅靠工程化流程难以解决 AI 驱动工作流的复杂性，需要更广义的上下文工程、治理和守则，才能实现可可靠扩展的开发。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/0f97b3f61763fd91a6eef9207a1d19e1f452ee11125771bbcbdbbb51a4c8600d/humanlayer/advanced-context-engineering-for-coding-agents' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/0f97b3f61763fd91a6eef9207a1d19e1f452ee11125771bbcbdbbb51a4c8600d/humanlayer/advanced-context-engineering-for-coding-agents' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nativ lets you run frontier open models locally on macOS, reducing reliance on cloud inference and enabling offline experimentation with privacy and faster iteration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Nativ 让在 macOS 上本地运行前沿开放模型成为可能，降低对云端推理的依赖，提升隐私保护和离线实验的灵活性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48980019' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.emergingtrajectories.com/lh/frontier-lab-economics/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kimi K3, Qwen 3.8, and Anthropic&#x27;s (Potential) Unravelling</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kimi K3、Qwen 3.8 与 Anthropic 的（潜在）瓦解</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48980019' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 365</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48980019' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 333</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Recent model launches like Kimi K3 and Qwen 3.8 are reshaping the frontier-model economics and intensifying competition, while Anthropic faces strategic and operational headwinds that could strain its position. The situation underscores how compute costs, alignment challenges, and rapid iteration are altering market leadership and incentives for big-model players.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kimi K3 和 Qwen 3.8 等新型号正在重塑前沿模型的经济学并加剧竞争，而 Anthropic 正面临可能动摇其地位的战略与运营压力。此局面凸显計算成本、对齐难题和快速迭代如何改变大型模型厂商的市场领导力与激励机制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.emergingtrajectories.com/assets/677d5ee3d185dd5dede0a75e/67a0d0ade0b78706efd6a5d8_heropink.png' data-full='https://www.emergingtrajectories.com/assets/677d5ee3d185dd5dede0a75e/67a0d0ade0b78706efd6a5d8_heropink.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49040296' data-tags='Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.dbos.dev/blog/postgres-listen-notify-scalability' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Postgres LISTEN/NOTIFY actually scales</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Postgres LISTEN/NOTIFY 真正具备可扩展性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49040296' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 363</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49040296' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New findings show PostgreSQL&#x27;s LISTEN/NOTIFY can scale under heavier workloads than commonly assumed. The write-up covers architectural tweaks and practical guidelines to sustain pub/sub style notifications at higher throughputs, including replication-aware patterns. That matters for real-time dashboards and event-driven systems that rely on Postgres as a backbone.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新发现表明 PostgreSQL 的 LISTEN/NOTIFY 在高负载下其实可以实现较好的扩展性。文中讨论了架构优化与实操要点，帮助在高吞吐场景中维持发布-订阅型通知，包含与复制相关的考虑。对依赖 Postgres 进行实时仪表盘和事件驱动架构的开发者尤为相关。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/672411cbf038560468c9e68f/6a62e3a5eb32f7dd71332477_Scaling-Postgres-Listen-Notify.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/672411cbf038560468c9e68f/6a62e3a5eb32f7dd71332477_Scaling-Postgres-Listen-Notify.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://asia.nikkei.com/business/technology/five-us-tech-giants-hidden-debts-soar-to-1.65tn-on-opaque-ai-funding' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Five US tech giants&#x27; hidden debts soar to $1.65T on opaque AI funding</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>五大美企在不透明 AI 融资下隐藏债务达 1.65 万亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48987863' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 362</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48987863' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 257</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Five US tech giants carry hidden debts totaling about $1.65 trillion tied to opaque AI funding arrangements, obscuring true leverage. The disclosure signals how AI investments and financing shapes corporate risk profiles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>五大美企的隐藏债务总额约1.65万亿美元，与不透明的 AI 融资安排相关，掩盖了真实杠杆与风险。此类投资与融资的结构正在重新塑造企业风险格局。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ft.com/v3/image/raw/https%3A%2F%2Fcms-image-bucket-productionv3-ap-northeast-1-a7d2.s3.ap-northeast-1.amazonaws.com%2Fimages%2F4%2F7%2F9%2F9%2F12819974-2-eng-GB%2F04ab7b8b57e0-photo_SXM2026071800000547.jpg?fit=cover&amp;gravity=faces&amp;dpr=2&amp;quality=medium&amp;source=nar-cms&amp;format=auto&amp;width=1260&amp;height=630' data-full='https://images.ft.com/v3/image/raw/https%3A%2F%2Fcms-image-bucket-productionv3-ap-northeast-1-a7d2.s3.ap-northeast-1.amazonaws.com%2Fimages%2F4%2F7%2F9%2F9%2F12819974-2-eng-GB%2F04ab7b8b57e0-photo_SXM2026071800000547.jpg?fit=cover&amp;gravity=faces&amp;dpr=2&amp;quality=medium&amp;source=nar-cms&amp;format=auto&amp;width=1260&amp;height=630' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(84) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://codeberg.org/Codeberg/org/pulls/1254' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Codeberg Bans Cryptocurrency Projects</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Codeberg 禁止加密货币项目</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49015588' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 362</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49015588' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 626</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Codeberg updates its policy to ban cryptocurrency-related projects, citing legal, ethical, and security concerns. The move highlights how open-source hosting platforms balance openness with risk management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Codeberg 更新政策，禁止在平台上托管与加密货币相关的项目，理由涉及法律、伦理与安全等风险。这体现开源托管平台在开放协作与风险管理之间的取舍。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thenextweb.com/news/ai-advice-suppresses-critical-thinking-wrong-answers-study' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI advice made people less accurate but more confident – sudy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 助言让人更自信却更不准？研究指出</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48971738' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 360</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48971738' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 207</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study finds AI-provided advice boosts confidence while reducing accuracy, highlighting risks of overreliance and the need for careful design of decision-support interfaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现，AI 提供的建议会提升自信但降低准确性，揭示过度依赖风险并强调在决策支持界面设计上的慎思。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49027892' data-tags='Science,Health,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(87) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/exclusive-death-girl-chinese-gene-editing-trial-was-never-made-public' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Couple pay &gt;$800k for a gene-editing therapy for their daughter. She died.</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>夫妻为女儿的基因编辑治疗花费超80万美元，最终去世</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49027892' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 357</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49027892' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 225</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Chinese couple spent over $800k on an experimental gene-editing therapy for their daughter, but she did not survive. The case spotlights the high stakes and unresolved ethical and regulatory questions around unproven, expensive treatments and the need for transparency in trial results.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一对中国夫妻为女儿的基因编辑治疗花费超过80万美元，但治疗未能挽救她的生命。此案凸显了高成本、未证实治疗的风险，以及试验透明度和监管伦理方面的紧迫问题。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48972970' data-tags='Gaming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(88) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/hgaiser/moonshine' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Moonshine: Lets you stream games from your PC to any device running Moonlight</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Moonshine：让你把 PC 游戏流式传输到任何运行 Moonlight 的设备</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48972970' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 353</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48972970' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 147</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Moonshine is a tool that enables streaming PC games to devices running Moonlight, expanding game streaming beyond official options. It highlights the ongoing ecosystem around Moonlight/GameStream, offering flexibility for multi-device play.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Moonshine 允许把电脑上的游戏流式传输到支持 Moonlight 的设备，拓展了官方 GameStream 的场景。此工具彰显了 Moonlight/GameStream 生态的活跃性，为跨设备低延迟游戏体验提供新的可能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/b1b9cbcc6dd911060ab3f1e658d7288902150aaffcab6515b9ba12f0e88462ab/hgaiser/moonshine' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/b1b9cbcc6dd911060ab3f1e658d7288902150aaffcab6515b9ba12f0e88462ab/hgaiser/moonshine' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48967256' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.minecraft.net/en-us/article/minecraft-26-3-snapshot-4' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Minecraft: Java Edition now uses SDL3</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Minecraft Java 版现已采用 SDL3</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48967256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 344</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48967256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 244</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Minecraft Java Edition has migrated its core to SDL3, replacing the previous multimedia layer. The shift modernizes the cross-platform stack and may reduce platform-specific bugs while enabling newer graphics and input features. This upgrade could pave the way for future performance improvements across Windows, macOS, and Linux.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Minecraft Java 版将核心迁移至 SDL3，替代旧的多媒体层。这一变更让跨平台栈更现代化，可能减少各平台的兼容性错误，并为引入新图形与输入特性创造条件。这或为 Windows、macOS 与 Linux 的后续性能优化铺路。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48965850' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(90) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/33972/files' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI reduces Codex Model Context Size from 372k to 272k</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 将 Codex 的上下文大小从 372k 减少到 272k</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48965850' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 343</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48965850' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 160</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Codex context window has been reduced from 372k to 272k tokens, impacting long codebases and prompts. Developers may need to restructure inputs or chunk code to preserve context, potentially affecting large-scale code generation tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Codex 的上下文窗口从 372k 减少到 272k token，这将影响处理较大代码库的场景。开发者可能需要重新组织输入或将代码拆分成块以维持上下文，进而影响大规模代码生成的任务。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/244841968?s=400&amp;v=4' data-full='https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/244841968?s=400&amp;v=4' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49005973' data-tags='AI,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(91) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.fiddery.com/businesses-with-ugly-ai-menu-redesigns/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Businesses with ugly AI menu redesigns</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>带有丑陋 AI 菜单重新设计的商业案例</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49005973' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 342</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49005973' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 260</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Businesses redesigning menus with AI can suffer UX pitfalls like confusing layouts or inconsistent tone. The piece argues for user-centered design, testing, and clear affordances to keep AI benefits tangible for customers. It highlights how design choices shape perceived value more than the underlying algorithms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>使用 AI 重新设计菜单的企业常陷入用户体验陷阱，如界面混乱或语气不一致。文章主张以用户为中心的设计、测试与明确的引导，使 AI 的优势对用户仍然清晰可感。强调设计选择对感知价值的影响往往大于底层算法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.fiddery.com/static/og-image.png' data-full='https://blog.fiddery.com/static/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48987822' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/janestreet/incremental' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Incremental – A library for incremental computations</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Incremental：一个用于增量计算的库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48987822' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 339</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48987822' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 67</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jane Street&#x27;s Incremental is a library that models dependencies and recomputations so only affected parts of a computation are updated, enabling efficient incremental workflows and reactive programming. It underpins complex dataflow systems and UI tooling by avoiding full recomputation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Incremental 是 Jane Street 推出的增量计算库，通过跟踪依赖关系实现只对受影响的部分重新计算，从而提升性能与响应性。它非常适合构建复杂数据流和响应式应用的增量工作流。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6b8ee0f140d53b9f54fe289d10d2259022d862f66563651a34a289b0ee7b9be2/janestreet/incremental' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6b8ee0f140d53b9f54fe289d10d2259022d862f66563651a34a289b0ee7b9be2/janestreet/incremental' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49063754' data-tags='Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(93) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nikolays.github.io/PGSimCity/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PGSimCity - How PostgreSQL Works</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PGSimCity：PostgreSQL 的工作原理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49063754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 339</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49063754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PGSimCity uses a city-building metaphor to illuminate PostgreSQL internals, from storage and indexing to the query planner and executor. It helps readers understand how MVCC, WAL, and background processes combine to make PostgreSQL reliable and scalable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PGSimCity 以城市场景比喻讲解 PostgreSQL 的内部结构，包括存储、索引、查询优化器和执行器。帮助读者理解 MVCC、WAL 等机制是如何协同工作，提升可靠性与可扩展性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nikolays.github.io/PGSimCity/og.png' data-full='https://nikolays.github.io/PGSimCity/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Half-Life 2 now runs natively on HaikuOS, showcasing progress in game compatibility and driver support for the niche OS. It demonstrates the vitality of hobbyist ecosystems and the potential for broader software ecosystems on smaller platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Half-Life 2 现已在 HaikuOS 上原生运行，展示了该小众操作系统在游戏兼容性与驱动支持方面的进展，体现了爱好者生态的活力以及小众平台上扩大软件生态的潜力。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(95) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dynomight.net/creatine/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Does creatine make you smarter?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>肌酸是否能让你更聪明？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49008642' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 333</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49008642' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 265</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reviews evidence on creatine and cognition, noting context-dependent benefits under sleep deprivation and cognitive load, with substantial individual differences and placebo considerations. Cautions against overgeneralizing promising results.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾肌酸与认知的证据，指出在睡眠不足和高认知负荷下可能有条件性的轻微益处，存在个体差异与安慰剂效应等问题，不应过度推广乐观结论。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://astral.sh/blog/ruff-v0.16.0' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ruff v0.16.0 – Significant new updates – 413 default rules up from 59</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ruff v0.16.0：重大更新，默认规则从59增至413</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49056112' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 332</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49056112' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 221</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ruff v0.16.0 ships a sweeping expansion of its default rule set, growing from 59 to 413 rules. The change tightens code quality out of the box, but teams may need to adjust configurations to avoid a flood of new warnings in existing projects. It highlights how linting tooling can shape coding standards at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ruff v0.16.0 发布，默认规则从59跃升至413，显著扩展了开箱即用的检查范围。改动提升了代码质量的一致性，但团队可能需要调整配置以避免在现有代码库中产生大量告警。这一更新反映了静态分析工具在大规模代码基准上的标准制定作用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://astral.sh/static/PNG/Ruff_v_0_16_0.png' data-full='https://astral.sh/static/PNG/Ruff_v_0_16_0.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(97) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://houseofsaud.com/irgc-claims-destroyed-amazon-bahrain-data-center/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>IRGC claims it destroyed Amazon&#x27;s Bahrain data center</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>伊朗革命卫队称摧毁亚马逊在巴林的数据中心</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49033240' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 326</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49033240' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 412</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An IRGC-linked claim alleges destruction of a data center in Bahrain, raising questions about attribution and the risk to critical infrastructure. Verification is unclear, but such statements amplify concerns about cyber violence and regional security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>伊朗革命卫队相关方声称摧毁巴林的一座数据中心，引发对归因和关键基础设施风险的讨论。尚无独立核实，但此类表述放大了对网络暴力与区域安全的担忧。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>回到 Kagi</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49006195' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 326</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49006195' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 250</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author revisits Kagi, a privacy-focused search engine, weighing its privacy protections, search quality, and user experience against mainstream options. The piece reflects on tradeoffs between privacy, convenience, and results relevance in modern search.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者重新评估以隐私为核心的搜索引擎 Kagi，权衡其隐私保护、搜索质量与用户体验与主流选项的取舍。文章反思在现代搜索中隐私、便利性与结果相关性之间的权衡。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49008396' data-tags='Science,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(99) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.mongabay.com/2026/07/zero-roadkill-as-amazon-canopy-bridges-secure-15000-crossings/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zero roadkill as Amazon canopy bridges secure 15,000 crossings</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>亚马逊树冠桥梁实现零车祸穿越，保障1.5万次动物跨越</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49008396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 325</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49008396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 98</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wildlife canopy bridges across roads in the Amazon have achieved 15,000 crossings with zero observed roadkill. This demonstrates how engineered wildlife corridors can restore habitat connectivity at scale. The result offers a compelling precedent for integrating ecological data with infrastructure design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>亚马逊林区的树冠通道让野生动物安全跨越公路，共记录了1.5万次穿越且未发现车祸。此举展示了通过工程化的野生动物走廊，在大规模范围内恢复栖息地连通性的可行性。该成果为将生态数据纳入基础设施设计提供了有力案例。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://imgs.mongabay.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/20/2026/07/20170143/4.-Macaco-aranha_Credito_Sergio-Leal-1-scaled-1-scaled.jpeg' data-full='https://imgs.mongabay.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/20/2026/07/20170143/4.-Macaco-aranha_Credito_Sergio-Leal-1-scaled-1-scaled.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48998362' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(100) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://terrytao.wordpress.com/2026/07/21/a-digestion-of-the-jacobian-conjecture-counterexample/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A digestion of the Jacobian conjecture counterexample</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对雅可比猜想反例的解读</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48998362' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 324</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48998362' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 134</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Terence Tao provides a detailed examination of a proposed counterexample to the Jacobian conjecture, explaining why it does not actually disprove the conjecture. The analysis highlights subtle issues in polynomial maps and their invertibility, illustrating why the problem remains open and what this counterexample teaches about current approaches.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>陶哲轩对一项关于雅可比猜想的反例进行了深入分析，解释其并不能真正推翻猜想。研究揭示了多项式映射及可逆性中的细微问题，凸显该问题至今仍未解决，以及该反例对现有研究方向的启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://secure.gravatar.com/blavatar/bd4bda4207561b6998f10dec44b570f04ff4072b20f89162d525b186dfca3e49?s=200&amp;ts=1784673675' data-full='https://secure.gravatar.com/blavatar/bd4bda4207561b6998f10dec44b570f04ff4072b20f89162d525b186dfca3e49?s=200&amp;ts=1784673675' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(101) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wsj.com/health/taylor-farms-cyclospora-recall-delay-call-41fef0bc' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Taylor Farms Called White House to Try to Delay Cyclospora Recall</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Taylor Farms 致函白宫，试图延缓 Cyclospora 召回</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49044074' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 322</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49044074' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 217</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A major produce producer reportedly asked the White House to delay a Cyclospora recall, highlighting tensions between public health safety and corporate influence. The episode raises questions about regulatory leverage, transparency in recalls, and supply-chain responsibility. It underscores how industry pressure can intersect with food-safety actions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，一家大型农产品公司曾请求白宫推迟环孢子虫召回，凸显公共卫生安全与企业影响力之间的张力。此事引发关于监管杠杆、召回透明度和供应链责任的讨论，显示行业压力可能影响食品安全决策。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49024643' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 309</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49024643' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 211</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author contends that the main objections to open-source AI are flawed and short-sighted. Open models can improve safety, transparency, and collaboration, while centralized systems risk lock-in and opacity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为反对开源 AI 的论点存在明显缺陷。开源模型能提升安全性、透明度和协作，而中心化系统则带来垄断和透明度不足的问题。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(108) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.phoronix.com/news/Firefox-153-Downloads' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Firefox 153 available with support for Vulkan video decoding, JPEG-XL</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Firefox 153：支持 Vulkan 视频解码与 JPEG-XL</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48978835' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 300</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48978835' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 76</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Firefox 153 adds Vulkan-based video decoding support and JPEG XL image format support, improving hardware acceleration and image quality. The update benefits users on capable GPUs and helps push newer codecs into mainstream browsing. It signals Mozilla&#x27;s ongoing performance and format diversification efforts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Firefox 153 新增 Vulkan 视频解码支持，并原生支持 JPEG XL 图像格式，提升硬件加速与画质。此更新对具备显卡加速的用户有利，并推动更现代编解码能力进入主流浏览器。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author explains stepping back from producing content to focus on quality, sustainability, and personal craft. <p class='hn-title'>(110) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://orionbrowser.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Orion Browser by Kagi</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kagi 推出的 Orion 浏览器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48970894' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 299</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48970894' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 299</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Orion Browser is a privacy-focused browser from Kagi that emphasizes tracker blocking and transparent privacy controls. It adds to a growing landscape of privacy-first browsers, signaling demand for a more private web experience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Orion 浏览器由 Kagi 打造，主打隐私保护，强化对追踪器的屏蔽并提供透明的隐私控制。此类隐私优先浏览器的崛起，反映出人们对更私密网页体验的需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://orionbrowser.com/orion-og.png' data-full='https://orionbrowser.com/orion-og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49020868' data-tags='AI,Business,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(111) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.axios.com/2026/07/22/openai-anthropic-open-models-trump-china' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI and Anthropic unite against open-weight AI risks to their bottom line</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 与 Anthropic 联手遏制开源权重 AI 风险以保护利润</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49020868' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 297</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49020868' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 331</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI and Anthropic are aligning to curb risks posed by open-weight AI that could threaten their business model or invite stricter regulation. The move signals a broader trend among major labs to balance openness with safety and commercial interests.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 与 Anthropic 联手降低开源权重模型带来的风险，以保护商业利益与潜在的监管压力。此举反映了大型实验室在追求开放性与安全、商业回报之间寻求权衡。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49022038' data-tags='Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(112) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://haqr.eu/tinyrenderer/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Software rendering in 500 lines of bare C++</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 500 行裸 C++ 实现的软件渲染器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49022038' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 293</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49022038' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A tiny software renderer demonstrates the core pipeline in about 500 lines of C++, including triangle setup, rasterization, and shading. It’s a practical reminder that many graphics fundamentals can be explored without a GPU, reinforcing how GPUs abstract complexity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个极简的软件渲染器用约 500 行 C++ 展示了三角形处理、光栅化和着色等核心管线。它提醒人们在没有GPU的情况下也能理解图形学基本原理，凸显了 GPU 对复杂性的封装作用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ziggit.dev/t/buz-a-drop-in-replacement-for-bun-using-modern-zig-with-sub-1s-incremental-builds/16891' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Buz – A fork of Bun using modern Zig, with sub-1s incremental builds</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Buz — 用 Zig 实现的 Bun 派生，具亚秒级增量构建</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49033099' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 292</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49033099' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 183</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Buz is a Bun fork implemented in Zig that promises sub-second incremental builds, accelerating developer feedback for JavaScript tooling. It aims to be a drop-in replacement with a modern, faster build pipeline.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Buz 是用 Zig 实现的 Bun 派生，承诺实现亚秒级增量构建，加速 JavaScript 工具链的开发反馈。目标是成为无缝替代品，提供更快的构建流水线。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(114) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://satoshi7190.github.io/Shinjuku-indoor-threejs-demo/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Shinjuku Station in 3D</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新宿站的 3D 模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48978792' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 286</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48978792' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A web demo renders Shinjuku Station&#x27;s interior in 3D using Three.js, showcasing large-scale indoor mapping and spatial navigation on the web. It demonstrates how client-side rendering and realistic modeling can help with wayfinding, simulations, and interactive visualizations of complex transit hubs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该网页演示用 Three.js 呈现了新宿站内部的 3D 场景，展示了网页端大规模室内地图与空间导航能力。该项目证明了客户端渲染与真实建模能在寻路、模拟和复杂交通枢纽的交互可视化中发挥作用。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48979120' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(115) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://var0.xyz/posts/perfection-is-not-over-engineering.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Perfection is not over-engineering</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>完美并非过度工程</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48979120' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 285</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48979120' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 122</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Perfection as a goal doesn&#x27;t automatically mean over-engineering; aiming for a well-polished, maintainable solution can be appropriate when it prevents costly rework or user friction. The key distinction is whether extra effort adds lasting value or just complexity—prioritize deliberate refinement over gratuitous features.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>追求完美并不等同于过度工程；在能避免重复返工或降低用户摩擦时，打造精致且可维护的解决方案是恰当的。关键在于额外投入是否带来持续价值，而不是徒增复杂度——应优先考虑有意的打磨而非无谓的功能堆砌。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49008512' data-tags='Hardware,Design,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(116) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.mit.edu/2026/three-sided-y-zipper-design-0504' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An old patent inspired the new &quot;Y-zipper&quot;, a three-sided fastener</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>旧专利启发的新型三边扣件：Y-zipper</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49008512' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 283</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49008512' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An old patent sparked the creation of the Y-zipper, a three-sided fastener designed for easier alignment and secure closure. The concept blends geometry and manufacturing considerations to improve one-handed operation and versatility in tight spaces. It shows how archival patents can spur practical hardware innovations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项旧专利促成了三边扣件“Y-zipper”的设计，旨在实现更易对齐与稳固闭合。该设计兼顾几何与制造考量，提升单手操作与在狭小空间中的灵活性。此案展示了存档专利如何激发实用的硬件创新。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.mit.edu/sites/default/files/images/202604/MIT-csail-YZipper.jpg' data-full='https://news.mit.edu/sites/default/files/images/202604/MIT-csail-YZipper.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Moonshot AI pauses taking new subscriptions to meet demand for its Kimi K3, hinting at strong market interest in their AI accelerator or product. The move underscores supply constraints in specialized AI hardware/software ecosystems and the challenge of scaling.</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gatwick Airport has started a robot-assisted parking service operated by Stanley Robotics, aiming to increase throughput and convenience for travelers. The system automates car parking to free up space and reduce manual handling, signaling a broader push toward on-airport automation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>盖特威克机场引入由 Stanley Robotics 运营的机器人停车服务，提升旅客停车体验与周转效率。自动化停车系统有望释放空间并减少人工操作，彰显机场自动化趋势。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(120) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/business/retail-consumer/alphabets-cash-burn-raises-alarm-big-tech-ai-spending-climbs-2026-07-23/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Alphabet&#x27;s cash burn raises alarm for Big Tech as AI spending climbs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Alphabet 的现金消耗因 AI 投资攀升引发对大型科技公司担忧</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49021006' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 273</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49021006' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 282</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Alphabet&#x27;s cash burn is rising as AI investment accelerates, raising questions about how quickly Big Tech can monetize these bets. The push weighs on margins and puts pressure on profitability timelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Alphabet 的现金消耗因 AI 投资加速而上升，引发对大型科技公司盈利能力的担忧。市场关注其如何在高投入与盈利之间实现长期成长。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49060856' data-tags='Programming,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(121) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://beyondloom.com/decker/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Decker, a platform that builds on the legacy of Hypercard and classic macOS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Decker：在 HyperCard 与经典 macOS 基础上构建的平台</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49060856' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 272</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49060856' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Decker reimagines the card-stack model from HyperCard for modern platforms, making it easier to prototype and assemble small apps. It blends classic macOS sensibilities with current capabilities to enable rapid, lightweight tooling and workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Decker 将 HyperCard 的卡片式、堆栈式应用模型带入现代平台，旨在简化小型应用的原型设计和组合。它在保留经典 macOS 风格的同时，利用当代能力，面向快速构建轻量工具与工作流。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48982535' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(122) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cursor.com/blog/agent-swarm-model-economics' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Agent swarms and the new model economics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>代理群与新模型经济学</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48982535' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 271</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48982535' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 139</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Agent swarms—coordinated collections of AI agents—could redefine model economics by sharing tasks, lowering per-task costs, and enabling new services that blend automation with decision-making.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>代理群（多智能体协同）可能通过分担任务、降低单任务成本，重新定义模型经济学，并推动新型自动化与决策服务的融合。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ptht05hbb1ssoooe.public.blob.vercel-storage.com/assets/blog/swarm-og.png' data-full='https://ptht05hbb1ssoooe.public.blob.vercel-storage.com/assets/blog/swarm-og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LearnOpenGL is a thorough, beginner-friendly resource that guides readers through modern OpenGL concepts with practical examples and progressively challenging projects. It’s a go-to reference for developers building cross-platform graphics apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LearnOpenGL 是一本全面、对初学者友好的资源，带你从现代 OpenGL 的核心概念入手，配以实战示例与渐进式项目，成为跨平台图形开发的权威参考。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DARPA and the Air Force conducted a test flight of an AI-controlled F-16, demonstrating autonomy in high-stakes flight and highlighting ongoing work to augment pilots with intelligent systems while preserving human oversight.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DARPA 与美国空军完成 AI 控制的 F-16 的试飞，展示了高风险飞行中的自主能力，同时强调在提高飞行安全的同时保留人机协作。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(125) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ecb.europa.eu/euro/banknotes/future_banknotes/html/all-design-proposals.en.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Future euro banknote design proposals</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>未来欧元钞票设计提案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49033110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 263</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49033110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 265</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The ECB lays out proposals for the next generation of euro banknotes, focusing on security upgrades, accessibility improvements, and sustainable production. The designs explore new visuals, tactile features, and anti-counterfeiting tech that will influence everyday cash handling and fintech interoperability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧洲央行公布未来欧元钞票的设计提案，重点提升防伪、无障碍性与可持续性。设计强调新视觉、触感特征与反伪技术，对日常现金流通及金融科技的互操作性产生影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(126) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.ktz.me/esp32-plane-radar/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An ESP32 based plane radar for my desk</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>基于 ESP32 的桌面飞机雷达</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49054107' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 259</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49054107' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A compact ESP32-powered radar project tracks small aircraft movement on a desk, offering real-time visuals from affordable hardware. It demonstrates how hobbyist sensors can mirror core radar concepts and be repurposed for education or dashboards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个用 ESP32 做的紧凑雷达项目在桌面上追踪小型飞机，能实时可视化，成本低廉的硬件就能实现。展示了爱好者级传感器如何复现雷达核心原理，并可用于教育或仪表板展示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pub-430e0c9ecb66464fa6999cd23a749d13.r2.dev/content/images/2026/07/esp32-plane-radar/img-3936.webp' data-full='https://pub-430e0c9ecb66464fa6999cd23a749d13.r2.dev/content/images/2026/07/esp32-plane-radar/img-3936.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(127) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vincentwoo.com/3d/grace_cathedral/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Immersive Gaussian Splat tour of grace cathedral, San Francisco</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN: An immersive tour of Grace Cathedral rendered with Gaussian splats, showcasing a novel 3D visualization technique. The project demonstrates how lightweight, GPU-friendly rendering can create rich, exploreable spaces without heavy poly meshes. It’s a taste of accessible, creative visualization for architecture and education.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN: 使用高斯斑点渲染实现的格雷斯大教堂沉浸式导览，展示了一种新颖的三维可视化技术。该项目证明了轻量级、对 GPU 友好渲染也能创造出丰富、可探索的虚拟空间，适用于建筑与教育场景的直观展示。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(129) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/fried_rice/status/2080059356322918777' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kimi K3 exploited the latest Redis server</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kimi K3 利用最新 Redis 服务器的漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49024938' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 257</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49024938' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 78</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A report notes that a researcher used the latest Redis server to demonstrate a notable exploitation, highlighting ongoing security considerations for in-memory data stores. It illustrates how vendor updates and patch cycles interact with exploit discovery. The takeaway is the importance of timely updates and careful exposure of interfaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道指出研究者利用最新的 Redis 服务器演示了一个显著利用案例，凸显了内存数据存储的持续安全议题。它说明了厂商更新与漏洞披露之间的博弈以及打补丁的时效性。结论是要及时更新并谨慎暴露接口。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(130) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://siepr.stanford.edu/publications/policy-brief/what-really-happening-jobs-separating-ai-hype-reality' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What is happening to jobs? Separating AI hype from reality</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 热潮中的就业现象：区分现实与炒作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49052570' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 256</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49052570' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 328</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Stanford policy brief examines current labor-market impacts of AI, distinguishing genuine productivity gains from hype and worker displacement. It outlines which sectors likely benefit, skills in demand, and policy recommendations like retraining and social safety nets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>斯坦福的一份政策简报分析 AI 对就业市场的真实影响，区分真正的生产力提升与炒作、以及对工人造成的岗位替代。指出受益行业、需求技能，以及对再培训和社会保障等政策的建议。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://siepr.stanford.edu/sites/g/files/sbiybj16606/files/styles/responsive_large/public/media/image/fig-1_0.png?itok=EDWKGtLu' data-full='https://siepr.stanford.edu/sites/g/files/sbiybj16606/files/styles/responsive_large/public/media/image/fig-1_0.png?itok=EDWKGtLu' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49050538' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(131) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/ng-interactive/2026/jul/25/flock-surveillance-cameras' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The growing vigilante movement to knock out Flock surveillance cameras</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>针对 Flock 监控摄像头的民间自卫行动日益增多</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49050538' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 253</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49050538' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 151</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vigilante groups are increasingly attempting to disable Flock surveillance cameras, intensifying the debate over privacy, safety, and the legality of counter-surveillance. The trend underscores how commodified, neighborhood-level security tech can provoke non-state responses and potential conflicts within communities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>针对 Flock 监控摄像头的民间自卫行动日益增多，推动了对隐私、公共安全和反监控行动合法性的讨论。此趋势凸显了面向社区的安防技术如何催生非官方干预，并可能在社区内部引发冲突。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/a0e891a7ff70313f89b236d20509fdb2404a049b/0_495_2400_1919/master/2400.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=8ce6eb2b1ce8c618bf027c75fec00f7c' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/a0e891a7ff70313f89b236d20509fdb2404a049b/0_495_2400_1919/master/2400.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=8ce6eb2b1ce8c618bf027c75fec00f7c' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49007610' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(132) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/mkratsios47/status/2079933645888880708' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>“We have information that Moonshot distilled Fable for the development of K3”</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>‘月球计划’从 Fable 洗炼用于 K3 的信息</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49007610' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 253</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49007610' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 668</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post hints at internal use of a tool or framework (Fable) by Moonshot to develop K3, suggesting cross-project knowledge transfer and tool reuse within ambitious programs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章暗示 Moonshot 内部将 Fable 用于 K3 的开发，揭示跨项目知识迁移与工具复用在野心勃勃的计划中的作用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(133) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.help.roblox.com/hc/en-us/articles/49648939984916-Android-Remote-Attestation' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Roblox Officially Supports GrapheneOS</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Roblox 现正式支持 GrapheneOS（Android），提升移动玩家的安全性与隐私保护。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48998404' data-tags='AI,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(134) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tryai.dev/blog/ai-drawing-arena-colored-pencils-claude-gpt-grok' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&quot;Drawing&quot; the Mona Lisa with GPT-5.6, Claude, Gemini, and Grok</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT-5.6、Claude、Gemini 与 Grok 联袂绘制蒙娜丽莎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48998404' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 249</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48998404' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 103</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Artists and AI collaborate to render the Mona Lisa using multiple models—GPT-5.6, Claude, Gemini, and Grok—showing how different prompt styles and model architectures approach the same subject. The piece compares the outputs to reveal strengths and limitations of current generative systems in producing coherent, stylistically varied artwork. It underscores how cross-model experimentation informs tool design and prompts craft.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过让 GPT-5.6、Claude、Gemini 与 Grok 各自尝试绘制同一主题，展示了不同模型在风格、细节与一致性方面的差异。比较结果揭示了当前生成式模型在艺术创作上的优势与局限，并为未来的工具设计与提示工程提供洞见。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.tryai.dev/blog/ai-drawing-arena-colored-pencils-claude-gpt-grok/opengraph-image-yqks0s?3313ef8f7f360358' data-full='https://www.tryai.dev/blog/ai-drawing-arena-colored-pencils-claude-gpt-grok/opengraph-image-yqks0s?3313ef8f7f360358' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(135) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://radicle.network/nodes/rosa.radicle.network/rad%3Az2v9tRJz1oknFAqCSY5W5c76nVvm6' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bitchat is now on Radicle</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49047365' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 247</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49047365' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 135</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bitchat 已接入 Radicle 网络，实现去中心化的点对点托管与版本化协作，提升聊天相关内容的开放性与可控性。</p>
