<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48978350' data-tags='Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(136) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/led-light-pollution' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LEDs’ potential to save our night skies</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LED 有潜力拯救我们的夜空</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48978350' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 245</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48978350' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 187</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LED lighting can curb skyglow with proper shielding and warmer spectra, delivering energy savings without washing out the night sky. For cities, LEDs become compatible with both illumination and environmental goals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>若采用合适的遮蔽设计和暖色光谱，LED 照明可以降低光污染并实现能源节省，同时不破坏夜空美观。文章探讨了暖色温和更智能的控制等设计要点，帮助保护天文观测和生态系统，同时实现节能目标。对于城市来说，LED 能在照明与环境目标之间取得更好平衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=67480571&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C125%2C0%2C125' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=67480571&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C125%2C0%2C125' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Demonstrates an LLM with 28.9M parameters running on a microcontroller costing around $8, using quantization or sparse techniques. It signals progress in true edge AI, with tradeoffs in speed, memory, and capability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示了一款28.9M参数的语言模型在约8美元的微控制器上运行，通常通过量化或稀疏技术实现。这标志着边缘 AI 的实际进展，同时带来速度、内存与能力的权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48981206' data-tags='AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(139) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://unslop.run/blog/measuring-ai-writing-on-arxiv' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How we measured AI writing across arXiv, and where the measurement breaks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们如何在 arXiv 上衡量 AI 写作及其局限</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48981206' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 241</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48981206' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 168</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece details a method for measuring AI-generated writing in arXiv submissions and identifies key failure points in the metric, such as stylistic ambiguity and model trickery. It underscores the need for robust, multi-metric evaluation to avoid overclaiming AI capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中描述了在 arXiv 投稿中衡量 AI 生成文本的方法，并指出度量标准的关键局限，如风格混淆与模型欺骗等。强调需要多指标和稳健评估来避免高估 AI 能力。</p>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48961698' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(140) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://morethanmoore.substack.com/p/intel-starts-shipping-high-na-euv' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Intel Starts Shipping High-NA EUV Silicon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英特尔开始出货高NA-EUV芯片</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48961698' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 240</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48961698' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 109</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Intel has started shipping chips produced with High-NA EUV lithography, enabling smaller features and higher transistor density. The move marks progress in next-generation manufacturing and could reshape competition with TSMC and Samsung, despite the cost and complexity of High-NA tooling. It signals Intel&#x27;s renewed push toward cutting-edge nodes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英特尔已开始交付使用高数值孔径EUV光刻工艺制造的芯片，特征尺寸更小、晶体管密度更高。这一进展标志着下一代制造工艺的推进，或对与台积电、三星的竞争格局产生影响，尽管高NA光刻成本与产能仍是挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49006096' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(141) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dead.garden/blog/how-my-images-are-dithered.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How My Images Are Dithered</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49006096' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 237</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49006096' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post explains why dithering is used when exporting images with limited color palettes, detailing techniques to preserve perceived detail and avoid banding. It shares practical tradeoffs and tips for artists and engineers working with constrained formats.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中介绍了在有限颜色调色板下导出图片时为何要进行抖动处理，阐述了保持视觉细节与避免色带的技巧与权衡。并给出在受限格式下的实用建议，面向艺术家与工程师。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(142) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/demo-zexuan/liang-wenfeng-investor-meeting-2026-7-22/blob/master/%E6%A2%81%E6%96%87%E9%94%8B%E6%8A%95%E8%B5%84%E8%80%85%E4%BA%A4%E6%B5%81%E4%BC%9A-%E6%96%87%E5%AD%97%E7%A8%BF_1_18_translate_20260723201651.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DeepSeek pause fundraise after comments on compute gap to US leaked (transcript) [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DeepSeek 暂停融资：关于美方算力差距的评论被泄露（文字稿）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49052912' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 236</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49052912' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 178</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A leaked transcript reveals investor and company discussions about compute gaps relative to the United States, prompting questions about competitiveness and funding dynamics in AI hardware startups.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>泄露的文字稿显示投资者与公司讨论相对美国的算力差距，引发对 AI 硬件初创公司竞争力与融资动态的关注。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48993448' data-tags='Security,Politics,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(143) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/ice-to-pay-thomson-reuters-125-million-to-find-voter-fraud/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ICE to Pay Thomson Reuters $125M to Find Voter Fraud</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ICE 将向 Thomson Reuters 支付 1.25 亿美元以查找选民舞弊</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48993448' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 235</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48993448' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 146</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ICE has awarded Thomson Reuters a $125 million contract to help detect voter fraud. The arrangement underscores private-sector involvement in election security and data analytics, raising questions about data access, accuracy, and civil-liberties implications. As election integrity remains a hot topic, the deal highlights tensions between efficiency and oversight.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ICE 已向 Thomson Reuters 支付 1.25 亿美元以帮助发现选民舞弊。这笔合同凸显了私营企业在选举安全与数据分析中的角色，引发关于数据获取、准确性及公民自由影响的讨论。随着选举诚信成为热议话题，该交易暴露了提高效率与加强监督之间的张力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/0f/76/0f76b548-bc58-4f25-abc3-3f5ebca07da4/content/images/size/w1200/2026/07/voter-speech.png' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/0f/76/0f76b548-bc58-4f25-abc3-3f5ebca07da4/content/images/size/w1200/2026/07/voter-speech.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49060978' data-tags='Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(144) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theclimatebrink.com/p/the-strongest-el-nino-ever' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Strongest El Niño Ever</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>有史以来最强的厄尔尼诺现象</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49060978' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 235</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49060978' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 244</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This year&#x27;s El Niño may be the strongest on record, driving extreme weather worldwide. Its impacts ripple through agriculture, energy, and supply chains, underscoring climate risks amplified by warming trends.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>今年的厄尔尼诺现象可能成为有史以来最强的之一，推动全球极端天气。其影响波及农业、能源与供应链，凸显气候变暖放大自然循环的风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!KuFt!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F58159098-201a-4e2c-9756-fb2da105b0a7_6000x4000.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!KuFt!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F58159098-201a-4e2c-9756-fb2da105b0a7_6000x4000.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>JetZero appears to be an aviation-focused project or startup pursuing next-generation aircraft concepts with efficiency and/or low emissions goals. The post likely covers the technology direction, milestones, or partnerships shaping its trajectory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>JetZero 似乎是一家聚焦下一代飞机概念的航天创业项目，致力于提升效率和降低排放。该条目可能聚焦技术方向、里程碑与潜在合作对其发展轨迹的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48969200' data-tags='Education,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(146) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hashcloak.com/blog/tutorial-introduction-to-formal-verification-with-lean-(part-1)' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Introduction to Formal Verification with Lean Part 1</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Lean 的形式化验证入门（第1部分）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48969200' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 233</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48969200' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lean’s formal verification approach introduces readers to machine-checked proofs, modeling invariants, and leveraging dependent types to express properties. This primer explains why formal methods matter for correctness and security-critical code. It sets the stage for building reliable software through mathematical guarantees.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Lean 的形式化验证方法引导读者学习机器可检验的证明、建模不变量以及利用依赖类型来表达性质。该入门文章解释了形式化方法为何对确保正确性和安全关键代码至关重要，并通过数学保障奠定构建高可靠软件的基石。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://framerusercontent.com/images/YaDPTsgOmR4s4qr9J36QmjU8.png?width=4501&amp;height=4501' data-full='https://framerusercontent.com/images/YaDPTsgOmR4s4qr9J36QmjU8.png?width=4501&amp;height=4501' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(147) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.newyorker.com/culture/the-weekend-essay/the-voice-of-google' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Voice of Google</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌之声</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48980053' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 227</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48980053' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 130</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An essay about Google&#x27;s influence on voice technology and culture explores how voice interfaces shape user expectations, market power, and the company&#x27;s internal priorities. Voice systems&#x27; design choices—what they listen for, how they respond, and whose data trains them—have outsized effects on privacy, access, and the web&#x27;s future interactions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇关于谷歌对语音技术与文化影响的随笔探讨了语音界面如何塑造用户期望、市场力量和公司内部取向。语音系统在监听、响应以及训练数据来源上的设计决定，对隐私、可访问性和未来网络交互具有重要影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.newyorker.com/photos/6a590c32c2a2b9f979c58b31/16:9/w_1280,c_limit/Moscoso_TheNewYorker_Google_C_Web.jpg' data-full='https://media.newyorker.com/photos/6a590c32c2a2b9f979c58b31/16:9/w_1280,c_limit/Moscoso_TheNewYorker_Google_C_Web.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(148) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ciphertalk.substack.com/p/nobody-knows-what-a-used-gpu-cluster' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nobody knows what a used GPU cluster is worth</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>没有人知道二手 GPU 集群值多少钱</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48917135' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 227</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48917135' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 196</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece surveys the opaque market for used GPU clusters, highlighting price volatility, limited data, and the risk of depreciation for AI workloads. It implies buyers should scrutinize total cost of ownership and supply chains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章梳理了二手 GPU 集群市场的价格不透明、波动性高，以及折旧风险，强调在 AI 负载场景下需仔细评估总拥有成本与供货链。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49020545' data-tags='Data,Show HN'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(149) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://globaloilnetwork.staffinganalytics.io/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: I simulated closing the Strait of Hormuz on real oil trade data</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: 我用真实石油贸易数据模拟霍尔木兹海峡的关闭情景</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49020545' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 226</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49020545' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 104</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An experiment simulates what would happen if Hormuz were closed using real oil-trade data. It highlights potential disruptions to flows and price volatility and demonstrates how data-driven analyses can illuminate geopolitical risk. The approach shows how accessible data can model high-stakes scenarios without real-world disruption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过真实石油贸易数据，作者模拟霍尔木兹海峡被关闭的情景，揭示能源流动与价格波动的潜在冲击。该方法展示了数据驱动分析在地缘政治风险评估中的应用，以及在不产生现实冲击的情况下进行情景探测的可行性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(150) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://saffroncr.itch.io/katavatis/devlog/1534514/building-a-tiny-3d-renderer-for-a-tiny-handheld' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Building a Tiny 3D Renderer for a Tiny Handheld</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为迷你手持设备打造迷你 3D 渲染器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49010993' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 226</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49010993' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hands-on devlog about building a compact software rasterizer for a handheld device with tight constraints. It discusses optimization choices, low-poly techniques, and what building graphics software on small hardware reveals about the limits of indie development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>实操日志，讲述为受限的手持设备构建紧凑的软件栅格化渲染器。涵盖优化选择、低多边形技术，以及在小型硬件上开发图形软件所揭示的极限。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48969635' data-tags='Open Source,Business,Hardware,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(151) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.phoronix.com/news/Last-MPEG-4-Patent-Expired' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Last MPEG-4 Visual Patent Has Expired</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>最后一个 MPEG-4 Visual 专利已到期</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48969635' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 224</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48969635' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 75</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>With the last MPEG-4 Visual patent expiration, licensing barriers ease for developers and hardware makers, potentially boosting open implementations and lower-cost codecs. The change could influence licensing models and drive broader adoption of MPEG-4 Visual technologies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着最后一个 MPEG-4 Visual 专利到期，开发者与硬件厂商在许可方面的障碍将减少，可能推动开源实现与成本更低的编解码方案。此变动或促使对 MPEG-4 Visual 技术的更广泛采用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.phoronix.net/image.php?id=2026&amp;image=mpeg4_last_patent' data-full='https://www.phoronix.net/image.php?id=2026&amp;image=mpeg4_last_patent' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49021783' data-tags='Space,Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(152) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eso.org/public/news/eso2610/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Astronomers may have found the first exomoon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>天文学家可能发现首颗系外卫星</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49021783' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 224</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49021783' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 82</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Astronomers report evidence for a moon orbiting an exoplanet, a candidate exomoon. If confirmed, it would be the first known moon beyond the solar system and would open new questions about moon formation in other systems and how common such satellites are.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>天文学家报告了绕系外行星运行的卫星的证据，这是一个候选的系外卫星。如果被确认，它将成为首颗已知的系外卫星，为研究其他星系中的卫星形成及其普遍性带来新问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.eso.org/images/screen/eso2610a.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.eso.org/images/screen/eso2610a.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48999825' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(153) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.jimgrey.net/2026/07/21/its-a-shame-whats-happened-to-radio-3/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>It&#x27;s a shame what&#x27;s happened to radio</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>广播电台的现状令人惋惜</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48999825' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 221</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48999825' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 273</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece laments how radio has fallen from its central cultural role amid streaming, podcasts, and algorithmic playlists. It argues for preserving local broadcasters and exploring new formats that leverage radio’s strengths in real-time, community-focused storytelling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章惋惜广播在流媒体和播客主导的时代地位下降，缺乏本地化与即时性的优势被稀释。作者呼吁保护本地广播资源，并探索让广播在实时、社区化讲述中仍具价值的新形式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/blog.jimgrey.net/wp-content/uploads/2014/08/meonwzzq.jpg?fit=1200%2C800&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/blog.jimgrey.net/wp-content/uploads/2014/08/meonwzzq.jpg?fit=1200%2C800&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48985182' data-tags='Hardware,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(154) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://essenceia.github.io/projects/ethernet_switch_asic/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Designing an Ethernet Switch ASIC</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>以太网交换机ASIC的设计</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48985182' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 220</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48985182' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deep dive into the hardware design challenges of a modern Ethernet switch ASIC, from packet buffering and fabric bandwidth to power and thermal constraints. It covers architectural tradeoffs that influence latency, throughput, and cost, and may illustrate a path from RTL to tapeout. The takeaway is how system-level constraints shape data-center networking chips.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章深入解析现代以太网交换机 ASIC 的设计难点，涵盖分组缓存、交换架构带宽、功耗与热管理等要点。讨论了影响延迟、吞吐和成本的架构权衡，并展示了从 RTL 到流片的实现路径。核心在于系统级约束如何塑造数据中心网络芯片的设计取舍。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://essenceia.github.io/projects/ethernet_switch_asic/feature_floorplan.png' data-full='https://essenceia.github.io/projects/ethernet_switch_asic/feature_floorplan.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(165) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/columnists/2026/07/20/airbus-takes-flight-from-aws-what-happens-next-is-critical/5274109' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Airbus Takes Flight from AWS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>空客摆脱 AWS 云服务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48976682' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 208</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48976682' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 166</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Airbus is moving critical workloads away from AWS, signaling a strategic shift toward in-house or alternative cloud platforms for aerospace software. The migration tests how well sensitive aviation data can stay within regulated boundaries, and it underscores the challenges of multi-cloud strategies in safety-critical industries. The outcome could reshape how large hardware-heavy manufacturers balance cloud flexibility with control and compliance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>空客正将关键工作负载从 AWS 转移，显示其在航空软件领域寻求自有或替代云的平台。迁移测试了敏感数据在受监管边界内的留存能力，也暴露出安全关键行业在多云策略中的难题。该进展可能重新塑造大型制造商在云灵活性、数据控制与合规之间的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.theregister.com/5274151.jpg?imageId=5274151&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' data-full='https://image.theregister.com/5274151.jpg?imageId=5274151&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49025766' data-tags='Hardware,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(166) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://obsoletesony.substack.com/p/why-sony-cant-bring-back-classic-walkman' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why Sony can&#x27;t bring back its classic Walkman models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么索尼无法让经典 Walkman 重现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49025766' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 208</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49025766' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 222</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues nostalgia aside, Sony&#x27;s classic Walkman can&#x27;t be realistically revived due to supply chain constraints, licensing, and shifting consumer behavior. It highlights how modern wearables, streaming, and digital rights complicate a hardware comeback. The takeaway is that beloved devices may be unfeasible even for big brands.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尽管存在情怀，索尼很难真正让经典 Walkman 重新回归，原因在于供应链约束、授权与消费行为的转变。现代可穿戴设备、流媒体和数字版权也让硬件复兴变得更加复杂。结论是，即使是大品牌，也未必能让心头好的设备重现。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(167) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://english.mapn.ro/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Third Drone Shot Down in Three Days in Romanian Territory</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>罗马尼亚领土连续三天击落第三架无人机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49057248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 208</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49057248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 241</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Romania reports a third drone shot down on its territory in three days, signaling mounting border-proximity drone activity and ongoing air-defense vigilance. The incidents illustrate how drone security is becoming a real-world border issue.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>罗马尼亚边境近三日内第三次击落无人机，凸显边境区域日益活跃的航空威胁与空域监控压力。事件反映出无人机在区域安全与防务中的现实挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48991139' data-tags='Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(168) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.minneapolisfed.org/article/2026/new-homeownership-measure-puts-people-first' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New US homeownership measure puts people first</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新美国房屋拥有率衡量标准：以人为本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48991139' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 206</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48991139' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 354</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Minneapolis Fed proposes a new homeownership metric that foregrounds people rather than raw ownership rates. It includes affordability, tenure security, and access to credit to reflect lived experiences. The measure could influence policy by highlighting real barriers to homeownership and mobility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>明尼阿波利斯联储提出一种新的房屋拥有率衡量标准，强调个人经历而非仅看拥有比例。新指标纳入可负担性、居住稳定性和信贷获取等维度，力求更贴近家庭实际状况。该改革可能影响政策取向，突出解决住房可负担和流动性的问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.minneapolisfed.org/-/media/assets/articles/2026/new-homeownership-measure-puts-people-first/new-homeownership-measure-puts-people-first-key.jpg?rev=61fc78eb59024eae81d2b8b85a5084f7' data-full='https://www.minneapolisfed.org/-/media/assets/articles/2026/new-homeownership-measure-puts-people-first/new-homeownership-measure-puts-people-first-key.jpg?rev=61fc78eb59024eae81d2b8b85a5084f7' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(169) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stencil.so/blog/prewalk' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>You only need the frontier model for one single edit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你只需要前沿模型来完成一次编辑</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48916512' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 204</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48916512' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that for a single targeted edit, you can rely on a frontier AI model rather than building complex pipelines. It outlines practical strategies, like guiding prompts and minimal scaffolding, to achieve precise changes with one pass. The point is to simplify workflows without sacrificing quality.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张针对一次单点编辑，只需使用最强的前沿模型即可，无需搭建繁琐的流程。给出通过提示引导和最小化搭建的实用策略，以一次性实现高精度的修改，旨在简化工作流的同时保持质量。</p>

The project illustrates how small, focused tools can pay off in real-world workflows.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(176) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pkg.go.dev/golang.org/x/tools/go/analysis' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Go Analysis Framework: modular static analysis by go team</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Go Analysis 框架：Go 团队的模块化静态分析工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49057398' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 195</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49057398' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Go&#x27;s Analysis Framework is a modular toolkit for building static analyzers as reusable passes. It underpins the Go tooling ecosystem (e.g., Staticcheck) and lets teams compose custom analyses to catch bugs early without full builds.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Go 的分析框架是一个模块化的静态分析工具包，允许将分析逻辑拆分为可重用的分析阶段。它支撑 Go 工具生态（如 Staticcheck），让团队能够定制自定义分析，在无需完整编译的情况下发现潜在问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pkg.go.dev/static/shared/logo/go-white.svg' data-full='https://pkg.go.dev/static/shared/logo/go-white.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49051947' data-tags='Energy,Business,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(177) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/sodium-ion-battery-peak-energy' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GM Backs Sodium Ion Batteries for U.S. Grid Storage</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通用汽车支持钠离子电池用于美国电网储能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49051947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 191</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49051947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 92</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GM backs sodium-ion batteries as a viable path for U.S. grid storage, citing cheaper materials and domestic supply abundance. Sodium-ion cells trade some energy density for cost and supply-security advantages, potentially accelerating long-duration storage and reducing reliance on lithium-centric supply chains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通用汽车宣布支持钠离子电池用于美国电网储能，强调原材料更便宜且来源更分散。钠离子电池与锂电池相比能量密度略低，但在成本和供应安全方面具有潜在优势，或有助于扩大长时段储能部署并减少对锂等供应链的依赖。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/a-sodium-ion-prismatic-cell-battery-pack.jpg?id=67517723&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C16%2C0%2C234' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/a-sodium-ion-prismatic-cell-battery-pack.jpg?id=67517723&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C16%2C0%2C234' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Inflect-Micro-v2 is a compact voice model achieving full speech capabilities with only 9.36M parameters, challenging the idea that big models are always necessary. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Inflect-Micro-v2 在仅有9.36M参数的情况下实现完整语音能力，挑战“大模型=好”的常规认知。文章可能涉及训练效率、就地推理与对边缘AI的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(179) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.debian.org/vote/2026/vote_002' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LLM Usage in Debian: Three Proposals</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Debian 中的 LLM 使用：三种提案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49050859' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 191</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49050859' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 172</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Three proposals outline how Debian communities might approach LLM tooling, licensing, and safety in open-source packaging and user experience. The ideas balance freedom, security, and practical AI integration for Debian users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>三种提案描述了 Debian 社区在开源打包、许可与安全方面引入 LLM 工具的路径。思路力求在自由、安全与对 Debian 用户的实用 AI 集成之间取得平衡。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49060592' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(180) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://davidnicholaswilliams.com/its-not-empowering-to-hand-off-the-details/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>It&#x27;s not empowering to hand off the details</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>把细节交给他人并不等于赋能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49060592' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 191</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49060592' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 114</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Handing off every detail to users often leaves them without true control. Real empowerment comes from thoughtful abstractions, clear boundaries, and tooling that reduce cognitive load while preserving meaningful decision-making.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将实现细节简单交付给用户往往并不带来自主感，反而可能削弱控制力。真正的赋能来自高层次的抽象、明确的边界以及减少认知负担的工具，同时保留关键决策权。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://davidnicholaswilliams.com/image/davidnicholaswilliams_512.png' data-full='https://davidnicholaswilliams.com/image/davidnicholaswilliams_512.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(182) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ishmael.textualize.io/blog/ghost-cut/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ghost Cut – Or why Cut and Paste is broken everywhere</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ghost Cut——为什么复制粘贴在各处都断裂</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49007626' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 189</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49007626' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 146</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ghost Cut analyzes why cut-and-paste behavior is inconsistent across apps, arguing for better clipboard experiences and standardized APIs. It highlights UX pitfalls in everyday editing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章分析为何跨应用的剪贴板体验存在不一致，呼吁改进剪贴板 UX 与标准化 API。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48979931' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(183) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nothings.org/gamedev/ssao/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Corners Don&#x27;t Look Like That: Regarding Screenspace Ambient Occlusion (2012)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>屏幕空间环境光遮蔽：角落看起来不对劲</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48979931' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 188</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48979931' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 91</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Screenspace ambient occlusion (SSAO) approximations often produce visibly wrong shading in corners and tight crevices because they rely on limited depth-buffer sampling and heuristics. Understanding those failure modes explains why SSAO looks cheap in many scenes and points to better alternatives like multi-scale sampling, analytic AO, or geometry-aware methods for cleaner, artifact-free shading.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SSAO 通过深度缓冲采样和启发式方法近似环境光遮蔽，但在角落和狭窄缝隙处常出现明显错误。分析这些缺陷可以解释其廉价感，并指引使用多尺度采样、解析 AO 或几何感知方法来得到更干净无伪影的遮蔽效果。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nothings.org/gamedev/ssao/cornell.jpg' data-full='https://nothings.org/gamedev/ssao/cornell.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 展示 Cactus Hybrid——让 Gemma 4 学会识别自身错误，从而提升输出的安全性与可靠性。文中介绍了方法、训练信号及对减少幻觉的潜在影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49002166' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(188) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/chrislgarry/Apollo-11' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Original Apollo 11 Guidance Computer source code for command and lunar modules</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>阿波罗11号导航计算机原始源代码（指挥舱与登陆舱）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49002166' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 184</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49002166' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 57</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The full original Apollo 11 Guidance Computer source code for the Command and Lunar Modules has been published, offering a rare window into the software that steered humanity to the Moon. The code highlights the era’s tight constraints, interrupt-driven real-time design, and enduring lessons for modern embedded systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阿波罗11号导航计算机指挥舱与登陆舱的原始源代码已公开，为人们提供了了解引导人类登月的软件的珍贵机会。代码展现了那个时代对资源极限的紧逼、事件驱动的实时设计，以及对现代嵌入式系统的持续启发。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Unseasonably warm conditions trigger banana plants to fruit in the UK after years of dormancy, highlighting how climate variability can enable surprising agricultural outcomes. The anecdote underscores evolving plant behavior in temperate regions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>异常温暖的天气让英国雷利花园的香蕉在多年休眠后结果实，凸显气候变动带来的意外农作物现象。这一趣闻反映在温带地区植物生长行为的变化。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为真正的 AI 竞争力来自专注与落地执行，而非新奇，给团队将 AI 架构转化为可交付产品提供实用建议。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article analyzes a relay market that powers token resellers and fraudsters, explaining how intermediaries enable bulk trades, fake liquidity, and scams. It discusses risks for investors and potential safeguards or market reforms.</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纽约地下结构复杂且多变，从基岩到填土再到地下水，各类条件共同影响地基、地铁隧道等基础设施。文章强调了 NYC 的高难度地质条件对施工规划和韧性设计的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(198) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nesbitt.io/2026/07/21/end-of-options.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>git&#x27;s –end-of-options Flag</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>git 的 --end-of-options 标志</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48991882' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 175</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48991882' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 109</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Git introduces a --end-of-options mechanism that stops option parsing mid-command, allowing subsequent arguments to be treated literally. This makes it easier to forward arbitrary args to subcommands and simplifies scripting around git workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Git 引入一个 --end-of-options 机制，允许在命令执行中止选项解析后再传入参数，后续参数按字面意义处理。此特性有助于将任意参数转发给子命令，简化 Git 工作流的脚本编写。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nesbitt.io/images/boxes.png' data-full='https://nesbitt.io/images/boxes.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(199) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lukekanies.com/writing/building-on-atproto/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Building on ATProto</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 ATProto 上构建应用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49025984' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 174</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49025984' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 94</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ATProto is designed to enable interoperable, decentralized social apps. This write-up outlines practical patterns for building on ATProto, covering core concepts like authentication, data models, and tooling to help developers ship apps that can interoperate across services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ATProto 旨在为去中心化的可互操作社交应用提供基础。本篇文章给出在 ATProto 上开发的实用做法与模式，涵盖身份认证、数据模型和开发工具等要点，帮助开发者构建跨服务互操作的应用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lukekanies.com/images/Mastodon.png' data-full='https://lukekanies.com/images/Mastodon.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49045040' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(201) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arcprize.org/leaderboard' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ARC-AGI Leaderboard</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ARC-AGI 排行榜</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49045040' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 170</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49045040' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 143</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The ARC-AGI Leaderboard tracks how AI agents perform across a suite of tasks designed to probe general intelligence, offering a moving bar for progress. The latest standings reveal where current systems excel and where significant gaps remain in flexible problem solving.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ARC-AGI 排行榜展示了 AI 系统在一组旨在测试通用智能的任务上的表现，提供一个衡量进展的动态基准。最新排名揭示了现有系统在灵活解决问题方面的优势与明显的短板。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48974292' data-tags='Legal,Business,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(202) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://theconversation.com/when-can-a-power-company-take-your-land-for-data-center-infrastructure-284061' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When can a power company take your land for data center infrastructure?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>电力公司何时可以因数据中心基础设施征用你的土地？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48974292' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 169</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48974292' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 149</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>As demand for data center capacity grows, utilities may seek to use or acquire private land for grid support and cooling infrastructure. The piece outlines legal frameworks around eminent domain, compensation, and resident safeguards, as well as practical considerations for communities confronting siting decisions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着数据中心容量需求上升，公用事业公司可能会动用或征用私人土地以建造电网基础设施和冷却系统。文章梳理了征用权、赔偿以及对居民的保护等法律框架，并讨论社区在选址决策中的现实考量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.theconversation.com/files/747575/original/file-20260713-56-nlkqvq.jpg?ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;rect=0%2C675%2C7780%2C3890&amp;q=45&amp;auto=format&amp;w=1356&amp;h=668&amp;fit=crop' data-full='https://images.theconversation.com/files/747575/original/file-20260713-56-nlkqvq.jpg?ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;rect=0%2C675%2C7780%2C3890&amp;q=45&amp;auto=format&amp;w=1356&amp;h=668&amp;fit=crop' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49053708' data-tags='Space,Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(203) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.seti.org/news/alien-world-chemistry-found-inside-meteorite/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Alien World Chemistry Found Inside Meteorite That Struck New Jersey Home</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>坠落新泽西的陨石中的外星世界化学</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49053708' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 169</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49053708' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 62</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Analyses of a meteorite that struck a New Jersey home reveal chemical signatures consistent with materials from other planetary bodies, shedding light on the diversity of early solar system chemistry. The finding highlights how meteorites preserve exotic minerals and isotopes that inform planetary science.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对坠落在新泽西住宅的陨石进行分析后，研究人员发现符合来自其他星体的化学成分证据，揭示早期太阳系材料的多样性。此发现强调陨石如何保存外星化学信息，助力行星科学研究。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.seti.org/media/hk4lcjsh/figure-4.jpg' data-full='https://www.seti.org/media/hk4lcjsh/figure-4.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49019220' data-tags='Legal,Business,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(204) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/jul/23/eu-fines-google-for-competition-breaches-over-search-and-apps' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EU fines Google €890M for competition breaches over search and apps</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟因搜索与应用领域的竞争违规对谷歌罚款8.9亿欧元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49019220' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 168</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49019220' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 178</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The EU fines Google 890 million euros for competition breaches tied to search and apps, underscoring Brussels&#x27; willingness to police anti-competitive practices in core products. The ruling adds to a regulatory backdrop pressing platform owners toward fairer access and transparency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟因谷歌在搜索与应用领域的竞争违规处以8.9亿欧元罚款，显示监管机构持续打击平台垄断。此案反映出对公平接入和透明度的日益关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/6e0272bc008856e02d4a2739ad877c01f88e273c/1493_528_4133_3309/master/4133.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=dd2dde729f6acfb4cb7c3ea96bbc11ad' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/6e0272bc008856e02d4a2739ad877c01f88e273c/1493_528_4133_3309/master/4133.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=dd2dde729f6acfb4cb7c3ea96bbc11ad' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49050152' data-tags='Security,Privacy,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(205) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.adafruit.com/2026/03/05/tiles-security-is-so-bad-its-a-feature-for-stalkers/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tile&#x27;s security is so bad it&#x27;s a feature for stalkers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tile 安全性糟糕，成为跟踪者可利用的隐患</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49050152' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 164</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49050152' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Security research shows Tile trackers exhibit privacy flaws that can be exploited by stalkers, including persistent identifiers and weak default protections. The findings underscore the responsibility of hardware makers to harden defaults and empower users to disable or randomize location data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究表明 Tile 追踪器存在隐私漏洞，可能被跟踪者利用，其中包括持续的唯一标识和薄弱的默认保护。研究强调厂商需要加强默认设置并为用户提供更强的定位数据控制能力，以防止滥用。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49026933' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(206) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5F-2Y1LPRek' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fil-C: Garbage In, Memory Safety Out [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Fil-C：垃圾入，内存安全出 [视频]</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49026933' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 164</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49026933' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 162</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains Fil-C, summarized as garbage in, memory safety out, via a video. Likely discusses how design decisions or tooling can enforce memory safety even with imperfect inputs, with implications for language design and runtime checks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>视频探讨 Fil-C 这一概念，强调在潜在不安全输入下仍能实现内存安全的设计思路及实现要点，对语言和运行时安全有启示。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49034948' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(207) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.patreon.com/jackconte/posts/painful-update-164628951' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Patreon laying off 20% of staff</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Patreon 裁员约 20% 的员工</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49034948' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 164</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49034948' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 270</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Patreon confirms a 20% layoff, a painful step CEO Jack Conte frames as necessary to align costs with a tougher funding climate. The move underscores how even established creator platforms are exposed to macro shifts, potentially delaying roadmap plans and changing how the company supports creators. It highlights the need for sustainable unit economics in the creator economy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Patreon 宣布裁员约 20%，CEO 透露这是在艰难经济环境下为控制成本所做的必要调整。此举反映出即使是成熟的创作者平台也易受宏观环境波动影响，短期或影响产品路线与对创作者的支持，凸显建立可持续成本结构的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49003386' data-tags='Open Source,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(208) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://codeberg.org/Codeberg/org/pulls/1253#issuecomment-19820434' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Codeberg bans vibe coded projects</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Codeberg 禁止 vibe-coded 项目</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49003386' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 164</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49003386' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 246</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Codeberg has barred projects tagged as vibe-coded, signaling platform governance intersecting with how projects are categorized.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Codeberg 已禁止被标记为 vibe-coded 的项目，体现了平台治理在项目分类上的影响力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://codeberg.org/Codeberg/org/pulls/1253/summary-card' data-full='https://codeberg.org/Codeberg/org/pulls/1253/summary-card' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48984231' data-tags='Privacy,Politics,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(209) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.scottrlarson.com/presentations/overcoming-surveillance-capitalism-with-awareness/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Power of Awareness: Overcoming Surveillance Capitalism</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>觉察的力量：走出监控资本主义</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48984231' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 163</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48984231' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The talk argues that awareness about data practices is a first step toward reclaiming agency in a surveillance economy. It offers practical routes—privacy-preserving tech, policy advocacy, and mindful consumption—to push back against pervasive data harvesting. The message is timely as AI-driven services increasingly shape daily life and business.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该演讲强调了解数据收集与使用的本质是重获自主权的第一步。它提出实际路径，如隐私保护技术、政策倡导和克制性使用经济中的谨慎选择，帮助人们抵御普遍的数据收集。信息在 AI 驱动的服务日益渗透生活和商业时尤为重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.scottrlarson.com/img/presentations/sur-cap/whatyoubecomedependsonovercome.jpg' data-full='https://www.scottrlarson.com/img/presentations/sur-cap/whatyoubecomedependsonovercome.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48996197' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(210) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cacm.acm.org/opinion/ai-didnt-make-programming-easier-it-just-made-it-differently-difficult/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI makes programming differently difficult</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 让编程以另一种方式变得更难</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48996197' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 163</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48996197' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 141</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI doesn&#x27;t make programming easier; it changes the difficulty. The piece argues that AI shifts the challenge toward prompt engineering, tool reliability, and system integration, requiring new practices for testing and governance. It suggests the future of programming is about designing workflows that incorporate AI responsibly.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为 AI 并未真正让编程变得更简单，而是把难点转移到提示设计、工具可靠性与系统集成上。需要建立新的测试与治理方法来应对 AI 的输出。未来的编程将更注重设计能够负责任地融入 AI 的工作流。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48970772' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(211) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://justsomebody.dev/blog/mikrotik-home-router' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>HomeLab #1: MikroTik as a Home Router</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在第一期 HomeLab 中，作者演示了将 MikroTik 用作家庭路由器，讨论成本、功能及在家庭网络中的适用性与局限。文章包含设置步骤、安全性要点和对性能的预期，展示了一个可自建且功能强大的家庭网络思路。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(213) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.expats.cz/czech-news/article/czech-news-in-brief-for-july-20-2026-monday-top-afternoon-headlines' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Czechia moves to ban mobile phones in schools from September 2027</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>捷克拟自2027年9月起在学校禁用手机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48980700' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 158</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48980700' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 85</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Czech policy proposal would ban mobile phones in schools starting September 2027 to curb distractions and create a more focused learning environment. The plan raises concerns about enforcement, equity, and how to balance tech-free policies with digital literacy. It signals a broader trend of tech-limiting rules in education.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>捷克拟自2027年9月起在学校全面禁用手机，以减少干扰并营造更专注的学习环境。该政策引发关于执行、教育公平以及如何在无手机环境中兼顾数字素养的担忧，折射出教育领域关于科技使用的持续讨论。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48974476' data-tags='Open Source,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(214) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.documentfoundation.org/blog/2026/07/17/microsofts-main-tool-for-lock-in/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How proprietary formats have become Microsoft’s main tool for lock-in</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>专有格式成了微软锁定用户的主要工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48974476' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 157</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48974476' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 122</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft increasingly relies on proprietary formats to lock users into its ecosystem, making data interoperability harder and strengthening dominance in Office, cloud, and beyond. The piece argues that closed formats hinder competition and boost switching costs, with implications for open standards and competitors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出微软越来越倚重专有格式来锁定用户，使数据互操作性更难，巩固了在 Office、云等领域的市场支配。封闭格式抬升切换成本、阻碍竞争，对开放标准和竞争对手造成影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.documentfoundation.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/vecteezy_a-collection-of-popular-microsoft-office-applications_80916331-e1784283246560.jpg' data-full='https://blog.documentfoundation.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/vecteezy_a-collection-of-popular-microsoft-office-applications_80916331-e1784283246560.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(215) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://yummymelon.com/devnull/malleable-computing-emacs-and-you.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Malleable computing, Emacs, and you</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>可塑性计算、Emacs 与你</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Malleable computing argues for user-programmable, adaptable systems, with Emacs as a touchstone for extensibility. It sketches practical routes to making your tools moldable to personal workflows and research domains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>倡导“可塑性计算”，主张让系统可由用户在运行时塑形，Emacs 成为可扩展性的案例。给出把工具按个人工作流和研究领域定制的实际路径。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Fairphone 6 宽角摄像头的 Linux 实验性支持</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49012777' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 157</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49012777' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 72</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fairphone 6 gains experimental Linux support for its wide camera, signaling progress toward open driver ecosystems on consumer devices. This openness could broaden hardware customization and longevity for users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Fairphone 6 的宽角摄像头获得了实验性 Linux 支持，标志着开源驱动生态在消费设备上的进展。这样的开放性有望提升用户自定义和设备寿命。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48993059' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(217) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.exe.dev/claude-is-not-a-compiler' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Is Not a Compiler</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude 不是编译器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48993059' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 157</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48993059' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 162</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A technical critique arguing that Claude cannot serve as a compiler; while powerful for language tasks, it lacks the guarantees needed for compiling code. Developers should treat it as a code-generation aid, not a replacement for a compiler.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>技术分析认为 Claude 不能胜任编译器的角色；尽管在语言任务上很强大，但在编译代码时缺乏所需的保证。开发者应把它当作辅助生成代码的工具，而非编译器的替代品。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.exe.dev/static/og-card.png' data-full='https://blog.exe.dev/static/og-card.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>那篇帖子并不存在，别再信任它</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48982374' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 156</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48982374' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 168</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An examination of a viral claim built around a post that never existed, illustrating how misinformation can spread when sources aren’t verified. It urges readers to fact-check and distrust unverified claims, even from seemingly credible channels. The piece underscores the importance of verifying information against primary sources.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对一个从未存在过的帖子所引发的错误信息进行解析，揭示在未核验来源时，错误信息如何传播。文章敦促读者进行事实核查，避免盲目信任看似可信的来源。强调对信息进行原始来源核对的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位77岁的共和党男子独自对 Flock 监控摄像头发起抗议，凸显了地方监控的隐私辩论。此举显示个人行动也能放大对公共场所安防科技的担忧并影响地方话语。</p>
