<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48981665' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(222) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bytecodealliance.org/articles/wasmtime-gc' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GC and Exceptions in Wasmtime</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Wasmtime 的 GC 与异常机制</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48981665' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 149</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48981665' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wasmtime’s discussion of GC and exceptions explains how WebAssembly’s future garbage collection and exception handling features fit into the runtime. It’s a signal of where WASM tooling is headed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Wasmtime 正在探讨 WebAssembly 将来引入的垃圾回收和异常处理机制如何嵌入运行时。文章讲解了兼容性、内存管理的影响，以及 GC 与异常成熟后对性能与语言互操作性的潜在变化，折射出 WASM 工具链的发展方向。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bytecodealliance.org/images/avatar.png' data-full='https://bytecodealliance.org/images/avatar.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cruller, Bun&#x27;s Zig-based runtime, advances with the Zig 0.16 update, showcasing deeper integration with Bun&#x27;s JavaScript tooling. It highlights performance gains, better portability, and closer alignment with Zig&#x27;s ecosystem, signaling a more cohesive cross-language runtime strategy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cruller 是 Bun 的基于 Zig 的运行时，在 Zig 0.16 版本中实现了与 Bun 的 JavaScript 工具链的更深整合，强调性能提升与可移植性。此举预示着跨语言运行时栈的协同演进，可能影响 Bun 的启动时间和模块加载效率。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Fedora 45 的香肠工厂</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49046525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 148</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49046525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critique of Fedora 45&#x27;s release process, using the ‘sausage factory’ metaphor to describe the churn of features, fixes, and QA that culminate in a new release. It highlights tensions between rapid shipping and maintaining stability in a community-driven distro.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以“香肠工厂”为比喻，批评 Fedora 45 的发布过程，描述功能整合、修复与质量保证的集约化流程。凸显在社区驱动的发行中，快速发布与稳定性之间的矛盾。</p>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49033087' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(225) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sliplane.io/blog/hetzner-inference' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hetzner is working on LLM Inference</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Hetzner 正在推进大模型推理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49033087' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 148</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49033087' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hetzner is building in-house LLM inference capabilities, detailing approaches to running large language models efficiently inside its data centers. The effort aims to offer cheaper, privacy-conscious alternatives to hyperscalers and to serve mid-market customers who want control over their data. It signals a broader trend of hosting providers expanding into AI workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Hetzner 正在自研大语言模型推理能力，计划在自家数据中心高效、可扩展地运行推理任务，以降低成本并提升隐私保护。此举可能为需要本地化推理的中小企业提供替代方案，体现了数据自治和低延迟方面的竞争力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(226) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fidget-spinner.github.io/posts/ultra-fast-tracing.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Python 3.15’s Ultra-Low Overhead Interpreter Profiling Mode</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Python 3.15 的超低开销解释器剖面模式</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48918140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 147</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48918140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Python 3.15 introduces an ultra-low overhead interpreter profiling mode, enabling more granular performance tracing with minimal impact on run-time. This helps developers optimize hot paths in large codebases and experiment with profiling in production-like environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Python 3.15 引入超低开销的解释器剖面模式，使性能跟踪在对运行时影响极小的前提下更细粒度。此特性有助于开发者对大型代码库的热路径进行优化，并在接近生产环境的场景中进行分析。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fidget-spinner.github.io/media/ultra-fast-tracing/interpreter-dual.svg' data-full='https://fidget-spinner.github.io/media/ultra-fast-tracing/interpreter-dual.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(229) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.statnews.com/2026/07/20/psychiatric-insurance-coverage-suicide-denial-personal-essay/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>My husbands suicide shows theres something very wrong with US insurance industry</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我丈夫的自杀暴露出美国保险行业的深层问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48981496' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 143</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48981496' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 159</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author recounts how her husband’s death exposed gaps and denial in U.S. psychiatric insurance coverage. The piece critiques the incentives and bureaucracy that hinder timely, adequate mental-health care and highlights how insurance design affects real-world outcomes for families dealing with mental illness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者回忆丈夫去世，揭示美国精神健康保险覆盖中的缺口与拒赔现象。文章批评行业激励与繁琐流程对获得及时、充分治疗的影响，强调保险设计直接影响家庭在面对精神疾病时的现实结果。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(231) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Saivineeth147/lora-speedrun' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LoRA Speedrun – a public wall-clock leaderboard for fine-tuning techniques</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LoRA 速度跑：用于微调技术的公开墙钟式排行榜</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48974325' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 142</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48974325' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LoRA Speedrun publicly catalogs wall-clock times for LoRA-based fine-tuning runs, enabling apples-to-apples comparisons of efficiency across models and setups. By surfacing how implementation details and hardware affect speed, it nudges researchers toward faster, more reproducible fine-tuning practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LoRA Speedrun 将基于 LoRA 的微调过程按墙钟时间公开排序，便于横向比较不同模型与配置的效率。该排行榜揭示实现细节和硬件选择对训练速度的影响，推动研究者追求更快、可复现的微调方法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1305115824/8a9e536e-cfd7-49ca-9feb-56cc68dc9815' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1305115824/8a9e536e-cfd7-49ca-9feb-56cc68dc9815' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(232) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://suzyahyah.github.io/machine%20learning/2026/06/27/trouble-with-time-series.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The unreasonable difficulty of time series forecasting</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>时间序列预测的非理性难题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48960582' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 142</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48960582' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Time series forecasting remains hard due to non-stationarity, regime shifts, and data limitations. The piece argues for humility in expectations and better evaluation practices for ML models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>由于非平稳性、情形转变与数据局限，时间序列预测依然困难。文章倡导对模型表现保持谦逊并改进评估方法。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(233) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://werd.io/private-healthcare-makes-industries-less-innovative-its-time-for-change/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Private healthcare makes industries less innovative. It&#x27;s time for change</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49021571' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 142</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49021571' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 159</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Private healthcare creates misaligned incentives and data silos that slow innovation across industries. The piece argues for reform—universal coverage, public funding for R&amp;D, and interoperability standards—to unleash talent and accelerate tech progress across sectors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为私人医疗体系的利润导向抑制了跨行业创新，提出改革诉求，如实现普遍覆盖、公共资金支持研发，以及提升互操作性标准，以释放人才潜力并加速科技进步。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(234) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://karimjedda.com/engineering-management-after-cost-of-code-collapse/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Engineering management after the cost of code collapsed</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>代码成本崩溃后的工程管理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49048212' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 141</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49048212' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 192</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>With the cost of code collapsing, engineering leadership must shift toward prioritizing maintainability, clear ownership, and automated quality gates. The piece argues for reorganizing teams, metrics, and incentives to curb long-term debt while preserving velocity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在代码成本崩溃后，工程管理需要把重点放在可维护性、明确的所有权以及自动化质量门槛上。文章建议重新组织团队结构、度量和激励机制，以降低长期技术债务并维持开发节奏。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://karimjedda.com/content/images/size/w1200/2026/07/meowtivate.jpeg' data-full='https://karimjedda.com/content/images/size/w1200/2026/07/meowtivate.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(236) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thedrive.com/news/how-texas-police-spent-4-5-million-on-four-chevy-tahoes' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Texas Police Spent $4.5M on Four Chevy Tahoes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>德州警方花费450万美元采购四辆雪佛兰 Tahoe</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48969582' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 139</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48969582' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece reveals that four Chevrolet Tahoe SUVs cost a combined $4.5 million, spotlighting procurement processes, pricing gaps, and potential inefficiencies in public safety spending. It raises questions about vehicle specs, lifecycle costs, and whether budget allocations align with community needs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章披露四辆雪佛兰 Tahoe 总价高达450万美元，聚焦采购流程、定价差距以及公共安全预算中的潜在低效。并提出关于车辆规格、使用周期成本以及预算分配是否符合社区需求的质疑。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(237) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hypr.land/news/update55/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hyprland 0.55 announced the switch to Lua for its config files</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Hyprland 0.55 宣布将配置文件切换为 Lua</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48982011' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 137</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48982011' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 130</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hyprland 0.55 introduces a switch to Lua for configuration, enabling more powerful, scriptable setups. The move promises greater customization and easier sharing of configurations, but may require rewriting existing setups. It signals a trend toward programmable desktops in tiling window managers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Hyprland 0.55 正式将配置改用 Lua，使用户能够通过脚本实现更强大的自定义。此举有望提升自定义自由度和配置的分享便利，但可能需要用户重写现有设置。标志着平铺式窗口管理器向可编程桌面的发展趋势。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48969530' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(238) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://etbe.coker.com.au/2026/07/19/ecc-ddr5/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ECC and DDR5</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ECC 与 DDR5</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48969530' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 136</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48969530' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 139</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DDR5 has more built-in complexity around error correction and refresh behavior, and ECC support varies between consumer and server platforms. Understanding how on-die ECC, module-level ECC, and motherboard/CPU support interact is important when choosing memory for reliability-sensitive workloads versus everyday consumer rigs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DDR5 在纠错和刷新机制上更复杂，而 ECC 支持在消费级和服务器平台间并不一致。选购内存时应该弄清片上纠错、模块级 ECC 与主板/CPU 的相互作用，以便针对高可靠性工作负载或日常消费机做出合适选择。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s0.wp.com/i/blank.jpg' data-full='https://s0.wp.com/i/blank.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48945804' data-tags='Web,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(239) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://screenwall.app/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ScreenWall – Turn old phones into synced widgets for your space</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ScreenWall——让旧手机变成跨房间同步的小部件墙</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48945804' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 135</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48945804' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ScreenWall lets you repurpose old smartphones into a synchronized, wall-like set of widgets across your space. It enables a low-cost ambient UI for home info — clocks, weather, calendars — with shared state across devices. It also addresses e-waste by giving old devices a second life.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ScreenWall 将旧手机整合成跨房间同步的小部件墙，提供分布式且低成本的居家信息界面，如时钟、天气、日历等。通过共享状态实现跨设备协同，同时也为电子废弃物再利用提供方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://screenwall.app/landing/screenwall-demo-poster.jpg?v=20260712a' data-full='https://screenwall.app/landing/screenwall-demo-poster.jpg?v=20260712a' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Law schools are rethinking curricula to address AI literacy, ethics, and risk management in practice. The piece outlines how curricula, accreditation, and interdisciplinary training may adapt to a world where AI increasingly intersects law.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法学院正在重新设计课程，以应对 AI 素养、伦理与风险管理的现实需求。文章探讨课程、认证与跨学科学习如何适应 AI 与法律交叉的世界。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(244) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ammoniaenergy.org/articles/flexible-renewable-ammonia-demonstrator-now-operational-in-minnesota/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Producing ammonia and fertiliser using wind power in Morris, Minnesota</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49050735' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 128</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49050735' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 98</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A wind-powered ammonia production and fertilizer demonstration in Morris, Minnesota demonstrates blending renewables with chemical manufacturing. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>明尼苏达州莫里斯的风能制氨与肥料示范项目将可再生能源与化工生产结合，探索在风力波动下维持工业过程的方法。此举旨在降低肥料生产的碳排放，并提升农村能源的韧性与自给能力。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英国 AISI/CAISI 对 Kimi K3 的网络能力给出初步评估，描述其当前功能及显著的缺口。报告为防务规划和新型网络工具的风险管理提供参考。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(249) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jointhefreeworld.org/blog/articles/emacs/emacs-eglot-scala-kotlin/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Escape IntelliJ: Scala and Kotlin LSPs on Emacs Eglot</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>跳出 IntelliJ：在 Emacs Eglot 上使用 Scala 与 Kotlin 的语言服务器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48990219' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 123</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48990219' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 99</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Emacs users can enable Scala and Kotlin language servers through Eglot, bringing IDE-like code intelligence to Emacs. The setup demonstrates how LSP tooling can rival traditional JVM IDEs for JVM languages without switching away from a lightweight editor, though parity with full-featured IDEs and startup cost remain considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Emacs 用户现在可以通过 Eglot 启用 Scala 与 Kotlin 的语言服务器，为 Emacs 增添代码自动完成、跳转与诊断等 IDE 式功能。此举证明了 LSP 工具链可以在轻量编辑器中提供 JVM 语言的开发体验，但与完整 IDE 的功能对等性和启动开销仍需权衡。</p>
</div>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(252) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://box2d.org/posts/2026/07/simd-for-collision/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SIMD for Collision</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用于碰撞检测的 SIMD 优化</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vectorizing collision checks with SIMD can dramatically boost physics performance in engines like Box2D, improving frame rates on mobile and desktops. The post details practical techniques for data layout and SIMD usage to accelerate broad-phase and narrow-phase tests.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过 SIMD 对碰撞检测进行向量化可显著提升物理引擎的性能，提升移动端和桌面端的帧率。文中介绍了面向数据布局和指令集的实用技巧，用以加速广义和窄域阶段的碰撞测试。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(253) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ai.google.dev/gemini-api/docs/latest-model' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gemini last models: temperature, top_p, and top_k are deprecated and ignored</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gemini 最新模型：温度、top_p、top_k 已弃用并被忽略</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48998606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48998606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google Gemini’s latest models no longer expose sampling controls like temperature, top_p, or top_k—the API simply ignores them. That shifts tuning responsibilities to model behavior and prompt design, making inputs more deterministic but less adjustable at runtime.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google Gemini 最新模型不再暴露采样控制参数 temperature、top_p、top_k，API 会直接忽略。调优将更多依赖提示设计与模型行为，变得更确定但在运行时可调性下降。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ai.google.dev/static/site-assets/images/share-gemini-api-2026-07.png' data-full='https://ai.google.dev/static/site-assets/images/share-gemini-api-2026-07.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(256) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.st-andrews.ac.uk/archive/sperm-whales-blow-bubbles-to-achieve-restful-vertical-sleep/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sperm Whales blow bubbles to achieve restful, vertical sleep</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>抹香鲸通过吐泡泡实现垂直睡眠</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49042751' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 118</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49042751' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers observe that sperm whales blow bubbles to maintain a vertical sleeping posture during long deep dives, enabling restful sleep. This reveals a unique adaptation in marine mammals that informs our understanding of sleep in extreme environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现抹香鲸在长时间潜水时会吐泡泡维持垂直睡姿，从而获得休息。此举揭示了海洋哺乳动物在极端环境中独特的睡眠适应性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.st-andrews.ac.uk/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/sperm-whale-vertical-rest-for-news-site-333x500.jpg' data-full='https://news.st-andrews.ac.uk/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/sperm-whale-vertical-rest-for-news-site-333x500.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48917906' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(257) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.labrujulaverde.com/en/2026/07/sealed-tomb-of-a-high-official-or-priest-filled-with-paintings-and-inscriptions-discovered-on-luxors-west-bank/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sealed tomb filled with paintings and inscriptions discovered in Egypt</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>埃及西岸封闭墓室内绘画与铭文被发现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48917906' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 117</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48917906' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 82</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Archaeologists uncovered a sealed tomb in Luxor&#x27;s West Bank containing paintings and inscriptions, offering new clues about the tomb&#x27;s occupant and the religious practices of the period. The find could enrich our understanding of funerary art and hieroglyphic texts, with potential names, titles, and ritual content awaiting study.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>考古学家在卢克索西岸发现一座封印墓室，内含绘画与铭文，或为墓主身份及当时宗教习俗提供新线索。这一发现可能丰富对葬礼艺术和象形文字的理解，后续研究有望揭示墓主姓名、头衔与仪式文本等信息。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.labrujulaverde.com/en/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/745886274_1442267391268136_4875976552273111574_n.webp' data-full='https://cdn.labrujulaverde.com/en/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/745886274_1442267391268136_4875976552273111574_n.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49034777' data-tags='Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(258) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tangled.org/oppi.li/stinkpot' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stinkpot: SQLite-backed shell history</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Stinkpot：SQLite 支撑的 shell 历史记录</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49034777' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 116</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49034777' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stinkpot stores your shell history in SQLite, enabling fast cross-session searches and rich querying. It makes it easy to discover repeat commands, quantify usage patterns, and drift from habitual workflows. This matters for developers who want deeper visibility into their own habits and faster command reuse.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Stinkpot 将 Shell 的历史记录存储在 SQLite 中，支持跨会话的快速检索和丰富的查询。可以方便地发现重复执行的命令、分析使用模式，以及识别常用工作流的改进点。对于想要提升工作效率、快速复用高效命令的开发者尤为有用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tangled.org/oppi.li/stinkpot/opengraph' data-full='https://tangled.org/oppi.li/stinkpot/opengraph' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Terence Tao&#x27;s ICM 2026 slides discuss how AI influences mathematics, including rigor, proofs, and education in an era of machine-assisted research.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(262) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnn.com/2026/07/15/science/medici-family-mystery-dna-malaria' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Medici family mystery may be solved after more than 400 years</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>梅第奇家族之谜或将在400多年后揭开</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49014007' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 114</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49014007' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ancient DNA analysis and malaria DNA from Medici-era remains point to a plausible explanation for a long-standing family mystery, offering a fresh narrative about illness and lineage. This case demonstrates how pathogen genomics can illuminate historical puzzles and reshape our understanding of aristocratic fate.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>古DNA与梅第奇时代遗骸中的疟原虫基因证据，可能为长期的家族之谜提供合理解释，揭示疾病在家族史中的作用。此研究展示病原体基因组学如何照亮历史难题，重新解读贵族命运的故事。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48952713' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(263) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hidden-phenomena.com/articles/rh' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What does the Riemann zeta function have to do with the distribution of primes?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>黎曼 ζ 函数与素数分布有何关联？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48952713' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 113</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48952713' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Riemann zeta function encodes prime distribution through its zeros: nontrivial zeros&#x27; locations control the error term in the prime number theorem via explicit formulas. The Riemann Hypothesis, which places all those zeros on the critical line, would tighten bounds on prime gaps and our quantitative understanding of primes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>黎曼 ζ 函数通过其零点将素数分布信息编码进显式公式：非平凡零点的位置决定素数计数误差项。若黎曼猜想成立，把所有这些零点约束在临界线上，将收紧关于素数间隔的界限并深化我们对素数分布的定量认识。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48953468' data-tags='Science,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(264) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/234286.1057820' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Birth of Prolog (1996)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Prolog 的诞生（1996）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48953468' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 112</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48953468' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece revisits the origins of Prolog, tracing how logic programming and rule-based inference shaped its design and early implementations. It discusses its influence on AI research and the enduring appeal of declarative languages, despite performance and tooling trade-offs. The historical vignette highlights how Prolog helped frame modern logic programming ideas.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾 Prolog 的起源，梳理其将逻辑编程与基于规则的推理融入语言设计及早期实现的过程。文章探讨它对人工智能研究的影响，以及尽管存在性能与工具链的权衡，宣称性语言的魅力仍然长期影响编程语言发展。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(265) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=NwhVJ7cv9B4' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cross sectioning insects in an electron microscope with a femtosecond laser [video]</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A video demonstrates using a femtosecond laser to cross-section insect specimens in an electron microscope, enabling precise, damage-minimized sample prep. The technique could streamline nanoscale imaging workflows in biology and materials science.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一段视频展示如何用飞秒激光在电子显显微镜中对昆虫样本进行横截切割，实现高精度、低损伤的制备，可能简化生物与材料科学领域的纳米级成像流程。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores programming languages designed to be written and read in two dimensions, where code can be laid out spatially to express structure and data flow.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨以二维方式编写和阅读的编程语言，使代码在空间中排布以表达结构与数据流。讨论这类语言在可视化方面的潜在优势，以及远离传统文本编码的挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49015735' data-tags='AI,Open Source,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(267) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://petals.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Petals: Run LLMs at home, BitTorrent-style</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Petals：在家运行大语言模型，点对点传输</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49015735' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 109</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49015735' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Petals enables running LLMs from home by distributing model shards via peer-to-peer networks, reducing reliance on centralized providers. It broadens access to large models while raising bandwidth, privacy, and governance considerations for DIY AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Petals 通过点对点网络将大型语言模型的模型分片分散在多台设备上，实现在家中运行，降低对中心化提供商的依赖。这让大型模型的获取更具可访问性，同时带来带宽、隐私与治理方面的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://petals.dev/logo.svg' data-full='https://petals.dev/logo.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(268) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://huggingface.co/spaces/webml-community/bonsai-webgpu' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>1-Bit LLM in the Browser</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>浏览器中的 1 位语言模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48936994' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 107</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48936994' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 1-bit LLM runs in the browser using WebGPU, demonstrating low-precision LLM inference without server side. The project showcases advances in on-device AI with privacy and latency benefits, highlighting how far browser-based ML has come.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过 WebGPU 在浏览器中实现的 1 位量化语言模型，展示了在本地进行低精度推理的可能性。该示例凸显了浏览器端 AI 的隐私与低延迟优势，体现对端到端模型推理能力的提升。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49021786' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(269) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.andros.dev/blog/06bfd107/emacs-is-a-lispboard/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Emacs Is a Lispboard</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Emacs is described as a Lispboard, highlighting its Lisp-based extensibility and the culture of customizing the editor for deep programming work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将 Emacs 比作 Lispboard，强调其基于 Lisp 的可扩展性以及为深度编程而定制编辑器的文化。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49007524' data-tags='Business,Finance,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(270) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://opennode.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenNode – Bitcoin Payment Processor</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenNode——比特币支付处理器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49007524' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 105</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49007524' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 87</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenNode provides a platform for merchants to accept Bitcoin with on-chain settlements and optional Lightning Network support. The service underscores growing infrastructure for crypto payments and developer-friendly tools. It’s part of a broader push to normalize crypto in everyday commerce.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenNode 提供商户接受比特币的解决方案，支持链上结算与可选的闪电网络。该服务体现了加密支付基础设施的日益完善与对开发者友好工具的重视，成为让加密货币正式进入日常商业的一环。</p>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49034217' data-tags='Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(271) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.andrewzigler.com/feed/the-case-for-muds-in-modern-times' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The case for MUDs in modern times (2018)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>现代仍需要 MUDs 的理由（2018）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49034217' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 104</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49034217' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 74</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Makes the case that Multi-User Dungeons (MUDs) remain relevant today as flexible, low-resource shared worlds that enable emergent gameplay and community building. The piece outlines technical and social benefits, arguing that MUDs offer value as lightweight, persistent environments in an age of heavy client-server apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张在现代仍然需要 MUDs，作为灵活且资源消耗低的共享虚拟世界，能促进涌现式玩法与社群建设。文中概述了技术与社交层面的优势，认为 MUDs 在当下庞大客户端-服务器应用的时代仍具价值。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.prismic.io/andrewzigler/591dfbb0-b88b-4d15-b09d-96a5c615df97_The-case-for-MUDs-in-modern-times.jpg?auto=compress,format&amp;rect=0,0,1200,628&amp;w=1200&amp;h=628' data-full='https://images.prismic.io/andrewzigler/591dfbb0-b88b-4d15-b09d-96a5c615df97_The-case-for-MUDs-in-modern-times.jpg?auto=compress,format&amp;rect=0,0,1200,628&amp;w=1200&amp;h=628' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(272) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cachebag.sh/journal/interactive-rebasing/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Git rebase -I is not that scary</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Git rebase -i 并不那么可怕</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49053385' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 104</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49053385' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 126</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Interactive rebasing isn&#x27;t as scary as it looks; with a few best practices, you can rewrite history, reorder commits, squash fixes, and resolve conflicts predictably. The piece shares practical workflows that help teams maintain a clean, understandable history.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>交互式变基并不像看起来那么可怕；掌握一些最佳实践后，可以有条理地重写历史、重新排序提交、合并修复并更可控地解决冲突。文章给出实用工作流，帮助团队维护整洁的提交历史。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(274) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.redfin.com/news/ai-data-centers-opposition-education-benefit/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Most Americans say &quot;not in my backyard&quot; to AI data centers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大多数美国人对 AI 数据中心说不（不在自家后院）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49007525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 103</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49007525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 216</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Public opinion leans against hosting AI data centers near homes, citing noise, energy use, and property values. Proponents argue local jobs and educational benefits, complicating siting decisions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>民众普遍不愿在自家附近设立 AI 数据中心，担心噪音、能耗和房产价值等问题。支持者则强调本地就业与教育收益，使选址决策更为复杂。文章强调社区关切在推动基础设施落地时与技术承诺同等重要。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(275) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/technology/safety-security/selfie-video-sign-in/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Introducing selfie for sign-in: a new way to access your Google Account</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自拍登录：一种更便捷访问 Google 账户的新方式</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google&#x27;s selfie-based sign-in offers a convenient authentication method powered by biometrics. It improves ease of access but raises privacy and security questions about biometric data and spoofing risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google 推出基于自拍的登录方式，利用生物识别提升认证便捷性。与此同时也引发对生物识别数据隐私和潜在欺骗风险的担忧。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(279) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/AARomanov1985/Audio-Cassette-Simulation' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Simulate cassette tape audio profiles using FFmpeg</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 FFmpeg 模拟磁带音频特性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49061887' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 100</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49061887' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A GitHub project demonstrates how to simulate cassette tape audio profiles using FFmpeg filters, capturing characteristics like frequency response and hiss. This enables retro-audio enthusiasts and preservation work to reproduce authentic analog textures in digital workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个 GitHub 项目展示如何用 FFmpeg 过滤器模拟磁带音频特性，包含频响、嘶声等模拟参数。对于复古音频爱好者和档案保护工作，这为在数字工作流中再现真实的磁带质感提供了工具。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49060295' data-tags='Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>

<p class='hn-title'>(288) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://postquantum.com/security-pqc/anssi-pqc-certification-2027/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>France&#x27;s Anssi Will Block PQC-Free Products from Certification Starting 2027</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ANSSI 将自 2027 年起对无 PQC 的产品实施认证阻断</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48994116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48994116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ANSSI will block PQC-free products from certification after 2027, forcing vendors to adopt post-quantum cryptography. The rule accelerates PQC migration and affects product roadmaps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法国 ANSSI 将于 2027 年起阻断未采用后量子密码学（PQC）的产品通过认证，迫使厂商采用 PQC。此举将加速 PQC 的普及并影响产品路线图。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://postquantum.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/ANSSI_Logo-1170x745.png' data-full='https://postquantum.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/ANSSI_Logo-1170x745.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(290) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/JuliaLang/julia/pull/62334' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>UnifiedIR for Julia</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Julia 的 UnifiedIR（统一中间表示）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48962600' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 95</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48962600' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A proposal to add a UnifiedIR to Julia aims to centralize backends under one intermediate representation, enabling cross-backend optimizations and future language features. If merged, this could simplify compiler maintenance and speed up experimentation with new targets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>为 Julia 引入统一的 UnifiedIR，意在把后端统一到同一个中间表示上，以支持跨后端优化和未来特性。若合并，将简化编译器维护并加速对新目标的尝试。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(291) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48981136' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Launch HN: Bloomy (YC S26) – AI-powered mastery learning for K-12</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Launch HN: Bloomy（YC S26）——面向 K-12 的 AI 驱动精通学习</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48981136' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 93</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48981136' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 99</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bloomy aims to deliver AI-powered mastery learning for K-12, personalizing content and pacing, with analytics for teachers. The YC startup signals growing interest in applying AI to scalable, outcome-focused education.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bloomy 旨在为 K-12 提供 AI 驱动的精通学习，通过个性化内容与节奏调控，以及教师分析工具来提升学习效果。这家 YC 初创公司体现了 AI 在教育领域扩大规模化应用的趋势。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>康奈尔的互动式鸟类墙</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cornell&#x27;s Interactive Wall of Birds blends biology, data visualization, and education to showcase avian diversity and migration. The project demonstrates how interactive displays can aid field research and public outreach, turning complex ecological data into accessible learning. It emphasizes open data and citizen science as engines for discovery.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>康奈尔的互动式鸟类墙将生物学、数据可视化与教育结合，展示鸟类多样性与迁徙。该项目体现了互动展示如何辅助田野研究与公众科普，将复杂的生态数据转化为易于理解的学习素材，强调开放数据与公民科学在发现中的作用。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece lays out practical bot-blocking techniques, from rate limiting and fingerprints to behavioral analysis and CAPTCHAs. It also cautions about accessibility, false positives, and the evolving landscape of bot-enabled abuse.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(301) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cruciblebench.ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Can a MUD evaluate LLMs? A $99 proof of concept</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MUD 能评估大模型吗？仅 $99 的概念验证</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49008538' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 91</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49008538' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 56</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A $99 proof-of-concept shows a MUD (text-based or multi-user dungeon) environment can be used to stress-test and evaluate LLMs in interactive, dynamic settings. It demonstrates how game-like sandboxes help reveal model weaknesses.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个 99 美元的概念验证展示了以 MUD 作为交互场景来评估大模型的能力，尤其是在动态、多人参与的环境中暴露模型弱点。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(302) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pme.uchicago.edu/news-events/news/cleaner-route-purifying-rare-earth-elements' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A solid-state “atomic channel” for separating rare earth elements</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用于分离稀土元素的固态原子通道</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49025831' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 90</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49025831' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers report a solid-state atomic channel for separating rare earth elements, potentially enabling cleaner, more energy-efficient purification. If scalable, this approach could reduce the environmental footprint of critical materials used in electronics and clean energy sectors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员提出一种固态原子通道用于分离稀土元素，或实现更高效、环境友好的纯化过程。若可扩展，这种方法可能降低电子、清洁能源领域关键材料的环境足迹。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pme.uchicago.edu/sites/default/files/2026-07/Chong_Liu_057.jpg' data-full='https://pme.uchicago.edu/sites/default/files/2026-07/Chong_Liu_057.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48987775' data-tags='Culture,Entertainment'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(303) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tokyodev.com/articles/vtubing-how-a-japanese-phenomenon-is-going-worldwide' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>VTubing: How a Japanese Phenomenon Is Going Worldwide</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>VTubing：日本现象正走向全球</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48987775' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 90</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48987775' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>VTubing is spreading from Japan worldwide as creators adopt virtual avatars, streaming tech, and monetization models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>VTubing 正从日本走向全球，创作者使用虚拟形象、直播技术和商业模式实现变现。文章分析了低门槛、粉丝文化和跨文化吸引力等驱动力。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49029538' data-tags='Education,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(304) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://visual6502.org/JSSim/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Visual 6502</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>The Visual 6502：交互式 6502 CPU 可视化仿真器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49029538' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 89</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49029538' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Visual 6502 is a web-based interactive simulator of the 6502 CPU, designed for learning and experimentation. It lets you step through instructions, view register states, and observe how code executes on a classic microprocessor. It&#x27;s a delightful resource for retrocomputing education and systems curiosity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>The Visual 6502 是一个基于网页的交互式 6502 CPU 模拟器，面向教学与实验。它允许你逐步执行指令、查看寄存器状态，并观察代码在经典处理器上的执行过程。是学习与体验复古计算的有趣资源。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(305) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ai.meta.com/blog/genesis-mission-lawrence-berkeley-national-laboratory-segment-anything-dino/?_fb_noscript=1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta&#x27;s AI models are powering the first wave of Genesis Mission projects</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48995074' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 87</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48995074' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 62</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta&#x27;s AI models are powering the first wave of Genesis Mission projects at Lawrence Berkeley National Laboratory and partners, enabling data analysis, perception, and automation tasks. This signals enterprise-scale AI integration into government and science workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 的 AI 模型正在为 Lawrence Berkeley National Laboratory 等合作方的 Genesis 任务项目提供初步支持，推动数据分析、感知与自动化等任务。此举预示着 AI 将在政府与科学工作流中逐步落地与扩展。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48947739' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(311) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/houslast3/85.30-GFLOPS-Single-Core-FP32-Matrix-Multiplication-on-AMD-Zen-3' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>85.3 GFlops: Optimizing FP32 Matrix Multiplication on a Single AMD Zen 3 Core</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>单核 AMD Zen 3 的 FP32 矩阵乘法优化到 85.3 GFLOPS</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48947739' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 83</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48947739' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A benchmark-style writeup shows achieving 85.3 GFLOPS for FP32 matrix multiply on a single AMD Zen 3 core. It covers techniques like vectorization, cache optimization, and instruction-level tuning, highlighting how much CPU microarchitectures can still surprise. Useful for performance engineers and hobbyists.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇基准式的文章展示在单个 AMD Zen 3 内核上实现 FP32 矩阵乘法达到 85.3 GFLOPS 的结果。文中讲解向量化、缓存优化和指令级调优等技巧，凸显 CPU 微架构仍有惊喜，适合性能工程师和爱好者参考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/05f32f20037c57f1e1abdf21f662bee5e7efad26b993c380d14443bf6c3056c2/houslast3/85.30-GFLOPS-Single-Core-FP32-Matrix-Multiplication-on-AMD-Zen-3' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/05f32f20037c57f1e1abdf21f662bee5e7efad26b993c380d14443bf6c3056c2/houslast3/85.30-GFLOPS-Single-Core-FP32-Matrix-Multiplication-on-AMD-Zen-3' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49063175' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(313) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/vercel-labs/scriptc' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Scriptc by Vercel: TypeScript-to-Native compiler, no JavaScript engine in binary</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Vercel 的 Scriptc：TypeScript 转原生编译器，二进制中不含 JavaScript 引擎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49063175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 83</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49063175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Scriptc compiles TypeScript directly to native binaries, eliminating the need for a JavaScript engine at runtime. It promises faster startup and leaner binaries, but raises questions about ecosystem compatibility and platform coverage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Scriptc 将 TypeScript 直接编译为原生二进制，不需要运行时 JavaScript 引擎。实现上可能带来启动更快、体积更小的优点，同时也带来生态兼容性与跨平台覆盖的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/31b1fe3badc657226d3889af7c0f3e4098a55d511e0bb8d99d51cc95603e9733/vercel-labs/scriptc' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/31b1fe3badc657226d3889af7c0f3e4098a55d511e0bb8d99d51cc95603e9733/vercel-labs/scriptc' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49007291' data-tags='Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(314) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.pypi.org/posts/2026-07-22-releases-now-reject-new-files-after-14-days/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PyPI Blog: Releases now reject new files after 14 days</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PyPI 博客：发布版本在14天后将拒绝新增文件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49007291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 81</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49007291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PyPI now rejects new release files after 14 days to reduce stale artifacts and security risk. Maintainers may need to re-upload if they intend to publish updates after the window.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PyPI 现在在发布后14天内拒绝新增文件，以减少陈旧资源与安全风险；若需在窗口外继续发布更新，维护者需重新上传。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.pypi.org/assets/images/social/posts/2026-07-22-releases-now-reject-new-files-after-14-days.png' data-full='https://blog.pypi.org/assets/images/social/posts/2026-07-22-releases-now-reject-new-files-after-14-days.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(324) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://energiesmedia.com/wind-turbine-stopped-electricity-wind-water-air/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wind turbine is being used to produce zero-carbon &quot;green ammonia&quot; fertilizer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>风力涡轮正在用于生产零碳“绿色氨”肥料</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49048819' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 76</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49048819' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 62</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wind power drives electrochemical or Haber-Bosch style ammonia production, enabling green ammonia for fertilizer without fossil fuels. This demonstrates how renewables can decarbonize chemical feedstocks and supply chains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>风力涡轮为生产零碳“绿色氨”肥料提供动力，推动不依赖化石燃料的氨制备。此举展示了可再生能源如何实现化学品生产的脱碳化，影响农业供应链。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://energiesmedia.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/7.-EM-In-Minnesota-one-wind-turbine-stopped-producing-only-electricity.jpg' data-full='https://energiesmedia.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/7.-EM-In-Minnesota-one-wind-turbine-stopped-producing-only-electricity.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>HMD Global releases information about a device called Touch 4G, signaling continued hardware development beyond smartphones. If real, the product would align with 4G-era positioning and show how vendors balance modern connectivity with legacy branding.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>HMD Global 公布了名为 Touch 4G 的设备，揭示其在 4G 时代定位的硬件产品线。当前信息有限，但此举反映了诺基亚品牌在硬件生态上的持续投入与多样化布局。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(326) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/3800646.3800650' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The drivers behind software delivery inefficiency</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>软件交付低效背后的驱动因素</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48982578' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 75</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48982578' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper identifies root causes of software delivery inefficiency—misaligned incentives, handoffs, and process bottlenecks—and suggests improving value streams, automation, and measurement to accelerate delivery.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论文揭示软件交付低效的根本原因包括激励错位、工作交接以及流程瓶颈，并提出通过优化价值流、增加自动化及更好衡量来加速交付的建议。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49048689' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(327) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pytorch.org/blog/bringing-pytorch-monarch-to-amd-gpus-single-controller-distributed-training-on-rocm/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bringing PyTorch Monarch to AMD GPUs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>把 PyTorch Monarch 带到 AMD GPU 平台</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49048689' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 74</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49048689' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PyTorch Monarch, designed to accelerate training and inference of large models, is now supported on AMD GPUs via ROCm. This expands access to high-performance AI workloads beyond NVIDIA hardware, helping AMD users run distributed training from a single controller. The move broadens hardware choices for large-scale ML teams and may spur more cross-vendor collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PyTorch Monarch 旨在加速大模型的训练与推理，现在通过 ROCm 获得对 AMD GPU 的支持。这扩展了高性能 AI 工作负载对非 NVIDIA 硬件的可用性，帮助 AMD 用户在单控制器下实现分布式训练。这一举措为大型 ML 团队带来更多硬件选择，并可能促进跨厂商协作。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pytorch.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/10/logo.svg' data-full='https://pytorch.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/10/logo.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(328) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49024620' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Launch HN: Screenpipe (YC S26) – Record how you work and turn that into agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Screenpipe（YC S26）——记录工作流程并转化为代理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49024620' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 74</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49024620' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Screenpipe records how you work and turns that into autonomous agents. The approach promises to automate workflows and boost productivity, but also raises concerns about data privacy and security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Screenpipe 记录你的工作流程并将其转化为可执行的代理，帮助自动化日常任务并提升生产力。此方法带来自动化的潜力，同时也引发数据隐私与安全方面的担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(329) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://happiness-science.org/price-of-happiness/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Price of Happiness (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>幸福的代价（2024）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48996364' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 72</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48996364' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This 2024 examination of happiness science distills how subjective well-being correlates with income, policy, and environment, offering updated measurements and cross-cultural insights. It highlights that happiness is influenced by more than money and underscores the importance of social and policy factors in well-being.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是对幸福科学的2024年研究，总结主观幸福感与收入、政策、环境等因素的相关性，并提供更新的测量与跨文化洞见。研究强调幸福受多种因素影响，社会与政策环境对福祉的作用不可忽视。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48971938' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 71</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48971938' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A large probabilistic computer uses stochastic approaches to derive meaningful answers from noisy data, illustrating how randomness-based computation can complement traditional AI workloads and improve resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这台全球最大的概率性计算机利用随机性从嘈杂数据中提取有用信息，展示了基于随机性的计算如何补充传统 AI 负载并提升鲁棒性。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Forth is a compact, stack-based language that maps well to how computers process instructions. The piece outlines simple, kid-friendly approaches to teaching Forth, with hands-on exercises that build intuition about programming without heavy syntax.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Forth 是一种简洁的堆栈式编程语言，能直观地映射计算机执行过程。文章给出面向儿童的实用教学方法与练习，帮助孩子在不依赖复杂语法的情况下建立编程直觉。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48980238' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(335) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.newyorker.com/science/annals-of-artificial-intelligence/there-is-no-ai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mythologizing AI makes it more likely that we’ll fail to operate it well (2023)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们没有 AI 的神话化：如何更好地运营 AI（2023）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48980238' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48980238' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 109</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that mythologizing AI—treating it as near-magic or sentient—undermines practical governance and safe deployment. It advocates rigorous testing, transparent capabilities, and sober risk assessment to avoid overreliance and mismanagement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张不要把 AI 陷入神话化，称其为近乎魔法或具备意识的说法会削弱实际治理与安全部署。提倡严格测试、透明的能力描述和务实的风险评估，以避免过度信任和管理失误。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.newyorker.com/photos/643ef9c0216f99acd11e5090/16:9/w_1280,c_limit/lanier_ai.jpg' data-full='https://media.newyorker.com/photos/643ef9c0216f99acd11e5090/16:9/w_1280,c_limit/lanier_ai.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This project distills all 253 patterns from Alexander&#x27;s Pattern Language into concise summaries, bridging classic design wisdom with modern design and software thinking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将 Alexander 的《模式语言》中的全部 253 条模式整理为简明摘要， bridging 经典建筑设计智慧与当代设计、软件思维。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(344) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.09369' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A taxonomy of omnicidal futures involving artificial intelligence (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>涉及人工智能的全灭式未来分类（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49029133' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 67</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49029133' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 57</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper classifies possible AI-driven futures that threaten existential outcomes, offering a taxonomy to structure risk discussions and policy work. It outlines different drivers of AI catastrophe, from autonomous decision-making to value misalignment, to guide researchers. It’s a useful framework for risk assessment in an advancing field.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论文将可能由人工智能引发的全灭级未来进行了系统分类，提供一个框架来组织风险讨论与政策工作。它梳理了从自治决策到价值错配等不同的灾难驱动因素，帮助研究者和决策者进行风险评估。对于快速发展的 AI 领域，这是一个有用的风险框架。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(347) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mariusbancila.ro/blog/2026/07/23/the-pimpl-idiom-and-the-cpp26-stdindirect-type/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The PImpl idiom and the C++26 std:indirect type</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49032095' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 65</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49032095' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 79</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Examines how the PImpl (pointer to implementation) idiom interacts with the new std::indirect type proposed for C++26, including implications for ABI stability, encapsulation, and code ergonomics. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文解析 PImpl 习语与 C++26 的 std::indirect 类型之间的关系。它讨论对 ABI 稳定性、封装性和可用性的影响，并给出在实际代码中结合二者的建议与常见陷阱。</p>
