<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49002762' data-tags='Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(350) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/jdefrancesco/dskDitto' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DskDitto: Ultra-fast, parallel duplicate-file detector</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DskDitto：极快的并行重复文件检测器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49002762' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49002762' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DskDitto delivers ultra-fast, parallel duplicate detection by exploiting multi-core CPUs, enabling scalable dedup across large datasets. It helps reclaim disk space, streamline backups, and can fit into build or CI pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DskDitto 通过多核并行实现超快的重复文件检测，能够对海量数据集进行高效去重。帮助释放磁盘空间、简化备份，并可无缝集成到构建或 CI 流水线中。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/3bcdda7b929c51cbaed74930bf0363a75e589d4b9c1118c9b639ec266576c5f8/jdefrancesco/dskDitto' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/3bcdda7b929c51cbaed74930bf0363a75e589d4b9c1118c9b639ec266576c5f8/jdefrancesco/dskDitto' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(351) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.onepercentbrighter.com/p/natural-experiments-prove-feeding' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Natural experiments prove phytoplankton carbon removal works</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自然实验证明浮游植物碳去除有效</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968701' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 63</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968701' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Natural experiments provide evidence that phytoplankton-driven carbon removal works, suggesting a viable nature-based approach to climate mitigation. The findings support pursuing ocean-based carbon sequestration while noting uncertainties around scale and ecological side effects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>自然实验显示浮游植物通过光合作用实现碳去除，支持以海洋为基础的气候缓解策略。研究强调海洋碳汇的潜力，但在规模化、成本与生态副作用方面仍存在不确定性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(360) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://magazine.sebastianraschka.com/p/controlling-reasoning-effort-in-llms' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Controlling Reasoning Effort in LLMs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在大型语言模型中控制推理努力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48979475' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48979475' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Controlling reasoning effort in LLMs explores methods to allocate compute and chain-of-thought selectively—trading latency and cost against solution quality. Techniques like early stopping, noisy prompting, or dynamic thinking budgets let systems conserve resources on easy cases while investing more compute on harder problems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 LLM 中控制推理投入讨论了按需分配计算和链式思考的方法，从而在延迟/成本与解答质量之间进行权衡。诸如早停、引入噪声的提示或动态思考预算等技巧，能在简单任务上节省资源，而在复杂问题上投入更多计算。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!jWb-!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F286a0beb-32b2-41fc-8bcf-6bae189b53f2_1488x840.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!jWb-!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F286a0beb-32b2-41fc-8bcf-6bae189b53f2_1488x840.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(364) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.harvardmagazine.com/research/harvard-human-evolution-genes-selective-pressure' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Humans haven&#x27;t stopped evolving</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人类从未停止进化</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49054307' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 59</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49054307' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 71</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New genetic research shows humans continue evolving under selective pressures, shaping traits like metabolism and disease resistance. The finding contextualizes ongoing biology with technology, medicine, and personalized health implications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新基因研究显示人类在持续经历选择压力而进化，影响代谢与疾病抵抗等性状。研究将生物学的持续演化放在技术、医学和个性化健康的语境中讨论。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(380) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theparisreview.org/blog/2020/01/23/the-silurian-hypothesis/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Silurian Hypothesis (2020)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>志留纪假说（2020）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49047563' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49047563' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 101</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This is a thought experiment about detecting signs of a pre-human civilization in Earth&#x27;s geological record, challenging what evidence of ancient technosignatures would look like and how hard it would be to recognize them. The piece imagines what signatures—if any—could survive, and what detection would imply for archaeology and SETI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个关于在地球地质记录中检测前人类文明迹象的思想实验，质疑古代技艺信号可能的证据 shape 以及被识别的难度。文章设想若留下痕迹，应该是什么样子，以及被发现会对考古学和地外文明研究意味着什么。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.theparisreview.org/blog/wp-content/uploads/2020/01/adobestock_46076619-1024x670.jpeg' data-full='https://www.theparisreview.org/blog/wp-content/uploads/2020/01/adobestock_46076619-1024x670.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(384) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.the-scientist.com/police-removed-prominent-scientists-from-the-ada-meeting-researchers-respond-74607' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Police Removed Prominent Scientists from the ADA Meeting. Researchers Respond</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>警方将 ADA 会议上的知名科学家驱离，研究人员回应</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49030474' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49030474' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Security at a prominent scientific meeting led to the removal of several researchers, triggering a swift response from the community. The incident underscores tensions around safety, protest, and academic dissemination at scientific gatherings. Researchers are calling for accountability and clearer guidelines for public events.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一次重要科学会议的安保措施导致多位研究人员被驱离，随后引发社区强烈回应。事件凸显科学聚会中安全、抗议与学术传播之间的紧张关系，研究人员要求加强问责并完善活动准入与安保规范。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49013995' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(386) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cacm.acm.org/blogcacm/if-you-think-you-can-do-real-world-text-to-sql/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Any text-to-SQL benchmark should address difficulties of real-world data stores</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>任何文本到 SQL 的基准都应解决真实世界数据存储的难题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49013995' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49013995' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that text-to-SQL benchmarks must reflect real-world data store challenges like complex schemas, data quality issues, and workloads. Without these realities, model evaluations risk overestimating practical capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张文本到 SQL 的基准需要体现真实世界的数据存储难题，如复杂模式、数据质量问题与实际工作负载。若缺乏这些现实因素，评估结果可能高估模型在实际场景中的能力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49062120' data-tags='Space,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(388) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/space-debris-atmosphere-burn-up' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Plasma Tunnels Reveal How Dying Satellites Fall to Earth</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>等离子体隧道揭示垂死卫星如何坠落地球</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49062120' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49062120' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers model how dying satellites interact with the upper atmosphere during reentry, revealing how plasma effects shape fragmentation and burn-up. The findings help refine debris risk assessments and inform future satellite design for safer end-of-life disposal.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者模拟垂死卫星在再入大气层时与等离子体的相互作用，揭示等离子体效应如何影响卫星碎裂与烧毁过程。这些发现有助于改进碎片风险评估，并为卫星安全退役设计提供参考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=67521175&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C156%2C0%2C94' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=67521175&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C156%2C0%2C94' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(393) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://freddiedeboer.substack.com/p/i-wouldnt-say-pangram-is-broken-but' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I wouldn&#x27;t say Pangram is broken, but I would say that it&#x27;s brittle</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我不会说 Pangram 已经崩溃，但可以说它很脆弱</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49023243' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49023243' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Author argues Pangram isn&#x27;t broken but is brittle, prone to edge-case failure as usage evolves. The piece suggests stronger typing, richer tests, and more robust tooling to reduce fragility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为 Pangram 不算是“崩溃”，只是比较脆弱，容易在边界用例或演进中出错。文中建议加强类型检查、增加测试覆盖，并考虑更稳健的工具以降低脆弱性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(400) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://exsitu.app/map' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Ex Situ – Open-source spatial index of displaced cultural artifacts</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Ex Situ——被流离失所文物的开源空间索引</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48988158' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48988158' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ex Situ provides an open-source spatial index for displaced cultural artifacts, mapping pieces across collections to aid provenance research and potential repatriation. The project emphasizes open data and community collaboration for cultural heritage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ex Situ 提供被流离失所文物的开源空间索引，将各馆藏中的文物位置聚合映射，帮助溯源研究与潜在归还。该项目强调数据开放与社区协作在文化遗产领域的作用。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(401) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://elsewhere.news/en/zhenfund/why-is-programming-so-captivating-yet-so-agonizing' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why is programming so captivating, yet so agonizing?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么编程如此吸引人却又如此痛苦？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49034468' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49034468' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Programming delivers deep problem-solving satisfaction and immediate feedback, but is often punishing due to edge cases, debugging drags, and burnout. The piece reflects on the emotional rollercoaster of building software and strategies to cope.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>编程带来深度的问题解决满足感和即时反馈，但常因边界条件、调试拖延和倦怠而变得痛苦。文章反思构建软件过程中的情感曲线，并给出应对策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://kkcktdhgzddmdjdjoenc.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/media/images/095cd835-7364-458b-b833-183e3e64c9a8/3e6840af1080.jpg' data-full='https://kkcktdhgzddmdjdjoenc.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/media/images/095cd835-7364-458b-b833-183e3e64c9a8/3e6840af1080.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(402) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.soofi.info/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Soofi – Sovereign Open Source Foundation Models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Soofi：主权开源基础模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48978192' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 47</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48978192' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Soofi proposes sovereign, open-source foundation models that governments or organizations can run and audit locally to avoid dependency on closed foreign providers. The approach emphasizes transparency, auditability, and control—trading off some performance or convenience for political and operational sovereignty.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Soofi 提出可由政府或组织本地部署并审计的主权开源基础模型，以规避对封闭外部厂商的依赖。该方案强调透明性与可审计性，愿以一定性能或便利性的代价换取政治与运营层面的自主可控。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.soofi.info/wp-content/uploads/go-x/u/4488c67c-9984-4f7c-a10f-5a087170b845/image-455x172.png' data-full='https://www.soofi.info/wp-content/uploads/go-x/u/4488c67c-9984-4f7c-a10f-5a087170b845/image-455x172.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(405) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.gao.gov/products/gao-26-108687' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Critical Minerals: Reducing U.S. import reliance with substitution and recycling</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>关键矿物：通过替代与回收降低美对进口依赖</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49008346' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 47</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49008346' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 73</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Summarizes a GAO report advocating substitution, domestic production, and recycling to reduce U.S. imports of critical minerals. It frames policy options, tradeoffs, and the importance for security and resilient supply chains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>概述 GAO 报告，主张通过替代材料、国内生产与回收等策略来降低美国对关键矿物进口的依赖，讨论政策选项、权衡以及对安全与供应链韧性的意义。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48908884' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(406) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.andr2i.com/posts/2026-07-06-eliminating-go-bound-checks-with-unsafe' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Eliminating Go bounds checks with unsafe</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 Go 中通过 unsafe 去除边界检查</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48908884' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48908884' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A write-up argues that using Go&#x27;s unsafe package can eliminate bounds checks, boosting performance in hot loops. It also discusses safety trade-offs and when such optimizations may be acceptable, underscoring how language features shape performance vs. safety decisions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中指出通过使用 unsafe 包可以移除 Go 的边界检查，从而在高频路径显著提升性能，同时要权衡潜在的内存安全风险。该分析强调语言特性在性能与安全之间的取舍。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.andr2i.com/assets/og-eliminating-go-bound-checks-with-unsafe.png' data-full='https://blog.andr2i.com/assets/og-eliminating-go-bound-checks-with-unsafe.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(407) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://louwrentius.com/i-think-you-might-be-fooling-yourself-with-ai.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I Think You Might Be Fooling Yourself with AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你可能在 AI 面前自欺自己</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49021843' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49021843' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that people often overestimate AI capabilities and misplace trust in automated systems. It urges clear-eyed thinking about limits, risks, and the need for human oversight in critical tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章认为人们常高估 AI 的能力，对自动系统过度信任。它主张对局限性、风险和在关键任务中需要的人类监督保持清醒认识。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://louwrentius.com/static/images/zfsnas01-small.jpg' data-full='https://louwrentius.com/static/images/zfsnas01-small.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(408) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simone.org/advertising/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What if we made advertising illegal?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>若广告非法化，会怎样？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49040989' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49040989' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Posits what would happen if advertising were banned, examining how digital platforms would fund content and services, and how user privacy and behavior might change.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者设想若全面禁止广告，探讨数字平台的盈利与可持续性将如何转型，以及用户隐私和行为的潜在变化。文中分析了从订阅制、公共资助到隐私友好商业模式等可能的过渡及其社会权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49020842' data-tags='Science,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(409) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://faculty.lsu.edu/kharms/files/flexner_1939.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Usefulness of Useless Knowledge (1939) [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>1939：《无用知识的用处》[PDF]</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49020842' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49020842' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Flexner&#x27;s 1939 essay defends the value of pure, curiosity-driven knowledge in science and culture. It argues that what seems &#x27;useless&#x27; today often fuels breakthroughs later, a timely reminder for research funding and long-term thinking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>1939 年的《无用知识的用处》主张纯粹知识与好奇驱动的研究对科学与文化发展至关重要。它提醒我们，当前看似无用的探索往往成为未来突破的催化剂。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49007581' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49007581' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An argument that ethics is the critical skill for technologists today, shaping responsible AI, product design, and policy implications. It offers practical guidance for integrating ethics into development workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张伦理是当下科技人员最关键的能力，影响负责任的人工智能、产品设计与政策层面。给出在开发流程中融入伦理的实际建议。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(411) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://platform-fools.com/posts/2026-04-27-nvlink/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When is NVLink worth it?</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post analyzes when NVLink&#x27;s high-bandwidth interconnect justifies the cost, considering model size, memory, and multi-GPU scaling, versus PCIe.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章分析在何种情形下 NVLink 的高带宽互连才值得投资，综合模型规模、内存和多 GPU 增长等因素与 PCIe 的对比。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(412) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.coindesk.com/markets/2026/07/24/brazilian-farmers-tokenized-dairy-cows-to-get-loans-bypassing-bank-lending-limits' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Brazilian farmers tokenized dairy cows to get loans, bypassing bank limits</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>巴西农民用奶牛代币化获得贷款，绕过银行放贷限额</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49048645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49048645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Farmers tokenized dairy cows as digital assets to secure loans, sidestepping traditional bank lending limits. The approach raises questions about collateralization, regulatory oversight, and the risks of tokenizing physical goods for finance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>巴西农民将奶牛进行数字化代币化以获得贷款，从而绕开银行对贷款的上限。此举引发对抵押品、监管和将实物资产代币化融资的风险等问题的关注。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(413) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://yang-ai-lab.github.io/Inertia-1/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Inertia-1: An Open Exploration to a Unified Motion Foundation Model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Inertia-1：统一运动基座模型的开源探索</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48978388' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48978388' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Inertia-1 is an open project aiming to build a unified foundation model for motion, covering tasks like trajectory prediction, animation, and physical simulation. By releasing code and checkpoints, it invites research and practical uses in robotics, animation, and interactive media where a shared motion representation can accelerate development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Inertia-1 致力于构建一个覆盖轨迹预测、动画与物理模拟等任务的统一运动基础模型，并以开源方式发布代码和权重。这个共享的运动表示可供机器人、动画和交互媒体等领域使用，从而加速相关开发与研究。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://yang-ai-lab.github.io/Inertia-1/static/images/wrist-transfer.svg' data-full='https://yang-ai-lab.github.io/Inertia-1/static/images/wrist-transfer.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49022668' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(415) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://papers.ssrn.com/sol3/papers.cfm?abstract_id=6959699' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Encryption and Globalization 15 Years Later: E2EE and the &quot;Going Dark&quot; Debate</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>十五年后：端对端加密与 Going Dark 辩论</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49022668' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49022668' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 15-year look at encryption policy shows that end-to-end encryption complicates law enforcement access, fueling ongoing debates about privacy, security, and reliability of digital communications. The paper reviews empirical evidence and policy trade-offs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对端对端加密与全球化的十五年回顾揭示，端对端加密使执法获取变得更困难，因此引发关于隐私、安全与数字通信可靠性的持续辩论。该论文评估了经验证据与政策权衡。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Trifle is an open-source analytics platform that stores answers rather than events. This outcome-focused approach emphasizes privacy and simplified analysis of user responses.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49000250' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(419) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://buildkite.engineering/how-a-flaky-test-exposed-a-redis-use-after-free/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A flaky test exposed a Redis client use-after-free</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一个不稳定的测试暴露了 Redis 客户端的释放后使用错误</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49000250' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49000250' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A flaky test reveals a use-after-free defect in a Redis client, highlighting memory-safety risks in high-performance data tooling and the importance of robust tests. The incident underscores why fuzzing and rigorous QA matter in low-latency systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个不稳定的测试暴露了 Redis 客户端的释放后使用缺陷，凸显高性能数据工具的内存安全风险以及对健全测试的需求。此事件强调了在低延迟系统中进行 fuzzing 和严格 QA 的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://buildkite.engineering/api/og/how-a-flaky-test-exposed-a-redis-use-after-free.png' data-full='https://buildkite.engineering/api/og/how-a-flaky-test-exposed-a-redis-use-after-free.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48989006' data-tags='Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(421) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://brightsec.com/research/detecting-ansi-escape-sequence-injection-in-mcp-servers-with-dast/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ANSI escape injection in MCP servers: Hidden from humans, visible to AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MCP 服务器的 ANSI 转义注入：对人类隐藏、对 AI 可见</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48989006' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48989006' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers reveal ANSI escape sequence injection in MCP servers that hides content from human operators while being detectable by AI-based analysis. The finding highlights a novel class of prompt/injection-style vulnerabilities in server interfaces and the need for sanitization and robust logging. This matters for security-conscious developers building AI-assisted tooling around game/server ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者发现 MCP 服务器存在 ANSI 转义注入漏洞，能让人类操作者看不到的内容与 AI 系统看到的内容不一致。此类“对人不可见、对 AI 可见”的攻击凸显了服务器接口的新型注入风险，强调需要更强的输入清洗和监控。对开发者而言，这也揭示了 AI 辅助的工具在在线环境中既是机遇也是攻击面。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://brightsec.com/research/detecting-ansi-escape-sequence-injection-in-mcp-servers-with-dast/images/Homepage-1-1024x576.jpg' data-full='https://brightsec.com/research/detecting-ansi-escape-sequence-injection-in-mcp-servers-with-dast/images/Homepage-1-1024x576.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitHub unveils a restructuring of its bug bounty program to streamline rules, broaden scope, and speed up payouts and triage. The overhaul aims to attract more researchers and clarify eligibility and reward tiers, signaling a stronger security posture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitHub 公布了漏洞赏金计划的重组，旨在简化规则、扩大覆盖范围并加速奖金与处置流程。此次改革希望吸引更多研究者参与，并明确合格条件与奖励等级，体现更积极的安全态度。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(426) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.jetbrains.com/go/2026/07/20/escape-analysis/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Escape Analysis in Go: Stack vs. Heap Allocations Explained</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Go 的逃逸分析：栈与堆分配解析</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48989065' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48989065' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Go&#x27;s escape analysis explains when allocations stay on the stack versus moving to the heap, with direct implications for latency and memory use. The post clarifies how the compiler makes decisions and how developers can optimize hot paths. It helps developers write more predictable, efficient Go code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Go 的逃逸分析解释了何时分配会留在栈上、何时会移动到堆上，以及这对延迟和内存使用的直接影响。文章阐明了编译器如何做出判断，以及开发者如何优化热点路径。帮助开发者编写更可预测、效率更高的 Go 代码。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48991945' data-tags='Programming'>

<p class='hn-title'>(431) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://differentinstruments.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PartialString – A finite-difference time-domain physical modelling synthesiser</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PartialString：基于有限差分时域的物理建模合成器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48991945' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48991945' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PartialString uses finite-difference time-domain methods to synthesize sounds via physical modeling, delivering acoustically grounded timbres. It showcases the trade-offs of FDTD-based synthesis—computational cost, stability, and integration with musical workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PartialString 采用有限差分时域方法进行物理建模合成，从而生成更符合物理直觉的音色。项目揭示了 FDTD 合成在计算成本、稳定性及与音乐工作流整合等方面的权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48973455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48973455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A curious 19th-century banknote featuring Satan illustrates how satirical and symbolic imagery entered monetary design as social commentary. The note is a historical oddity that reveals contemporary attitudes toward finance, religion, and political critique.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一张带有撒旦图像的十九世纪纸币展示了讽刺与象征性图案如何成为货币设计中的社会评论。这个历史奇闻反映了当时人们对金融、宗教与政治批判的态度。</p>

<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(443) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hothardware.com/reviews/amd-ryzen-7-7700x3d-gaming-cpu-review' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AMD Ryzen 7 7700X3D Review: 3D V-Cache Gaming Performance for Less – HotHardware</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AMD Ryzen 7 7700X3D 评测：3D V-Cache 以更低价格带来出色游戏性能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48966832' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 39</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48966832' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The 7700X3D delivers strong gaming performance thanks to 3D V-Cache at a compelling price point, with caveats around power efficiency and platform considerations. The review positions it as a solid choice for gamers seeking high frame rates without the premium of flagship parts. It also highlights how cache architecture can redefine price-to-performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>7700X3D 由于 3D V-Cache 在性价比上带来强劲的游戏性能，尽管在功耗与平台兼容性方面存在需要注意的地方。评测把它定位为寻求高帧率、又不愿承受旗舰部件高价的玩家的靠谱选择，同时强调缓存架构如何重新定义性价比。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hothardware.com/contentimages/Article/3619/content/16x9_2133x1200_highres-ryzen-7-7700x3d-review.jpg' data-full='https://hothardware.com/contentimages/Article/3619/content/16x9_2133x1200_highres-ryzen-7-7700x3d-review.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48985140' data-tags='Culture,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(444) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lcamtuf.substack.com/p/my-falling-out-with-the-rationalist' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>My falling-out with the rationalist community</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我与理性社区的分歧</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48985140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48985140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author recounts a falling-out with a rationalist community, reflecting on ideological rifts, governance, and norms in tech-focused circles. He argues that rigid purity tests and internal politics can stifle constructive critique, urging more humility and openness to dissent.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者回顾与理性主义社区的分歧，反思科技圈中的意识形态裂痕、治理与氛围。他认为僵化的纯洁性检验和内部政治会压抑建设性批评，呼吁更加谦逊与包容的沟通。此文折射出科技社群若采用更健康的对话方式将更具生产力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(445) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.appenmedia.com/opinion/letter-to-the-editor-the-creepiest-sales-demo-of-all-time/article_a8f0cc6c-29d3-41f7-8668-8a855f760efb.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The creepiest &#x27;sales demo&#x27; of all time</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>史上最诡异的销售演示</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48984555' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48984555' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A letter to the editor lambasts a sales demo for its creepy tactics, shining a light on ethical boundaries in tech marketing. It argues that sensational, intrusive demos undermine trust and invite tighter scrutiny of how products are pitched. The piece serves as a cautionary tale for vendors seeking to win business at the expense of user comfort.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>致编辑的信抨击了一场极其 creep 的销售演示，揭示科技营销中的伦理边界。其认为夸张、侵入性强的演示会破坏信任，引来对产品推介方式的更严格审视。此文提醒厂商在追逐销售时要兼顾用户体验与道德底线。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bloximages.newyork1.vip.townnews.com/appenmedia.com/content/tncms/assets/v3/editorial/a/8f/a8f0cc6c-29d3-41f7-8668-8a855f760efb/6a5e1cc713897.preview.jpg?crop=1763%2C926%2C0%2C124&amp;resize=1200%2C630&amp;order=crop%2Cresize' data-full='https://bloximages.newyork1.vip.townnews.com/appenmedia.com/content/tncms/assets/v3/editorial/a/8f/a8f0cc6c-29d3-41f7-8668-8a855f760efb/6a5e1cc713897.preview.jpg?crop=1763%2C926%2C0%2C124&amp;resize=1200%2C630&amp;order=crop%2Cresize' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(446) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Hikari-Systems/slater' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Slater – Low-memory graphdb designed for read-heavy graphs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Slater：为读密集型图设计的低内存图数据库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48996325' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48996325' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Slater is a graph database optimized for read-heavy workloads with a small memory footprint, useful for analytics pipelines that need large graphs on constrained hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Slater 是一个为读密集型工作负载设计的低内存图数据库，适合在受限硬件上处理大规模图分析。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49036136' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(447) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.3gpp.org/specifications-technologies/releases/release-19' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>3GPP Version 19</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>3GPP 第19 版</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49036136' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49036136' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>3GPP Release 19 formalizes new capabilities for 5G and beyond, including uplink improvements, enhanced MIMO, and network optimization features. The spec helps operators plan for higher throughput and more reliable services as networks mature.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>3GPP 第19版正式确立面向5G及未来的新能力，包括上行改进、增强型大规模MIMO和网络优化特性，为运营商在网络成熟阶段提升吞吐量与可靠性提供了规划依据。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.3gpp.org/images/3GU-icon.svg' data-full='https://www.3gpp.org/images/3GU-icon.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(448) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/newren/git-filter-repo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Quickly rewrite Git repository history</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>快速重写 Git 仓库历史</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48986703' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48986703' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Git-filter-repo is a fast tool for rewriting Git history, enabling tasks such as removing sensitive data, changing authors, or pruning large files with safer defaults than older methods. It includes practical examples and tips for patching history without breaking repos.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>git-filter-repo 提供快速、安全的历史重写能力，支持删除大文件、清除敏感数据、修正作者等操作，相较旧工具有更友善的默认选项。文中给出实用示例与使用要点，帮助在不破坏仓库的前提下改写历史。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/0d5de4062960bfbaa0123a9f425be26cc391615073a58538ed726c546df947cd/newren/git-filter-repo' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/0d5de4062960bfbaa0123a9f425be26cc391615073a58538ed726c546df947cd/newren/git-filter-repo' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(449) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://korben.info/en/gdid-windows-cut-tracker-vpn.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GDID Windows – Cut the tracker that follows you even under VPN</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GDID Windows：切断 VPN 下也会跟踪你的追踪器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48991984' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48991984' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A privacy-focused Windows tool to cut a persistent tracker that survives VPN usage. The feature reportedly reduces cross-vpn fingerprinting by eliminating identifiers that survive network tunneling. It underscores how traditional OS telemetry can erode privacy, and why dedicated privacy tooling matters.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个用于在 Windows 上切断在 VPN 下仍会跟踪用户的持久识别符的隐私工具。该工具能降低通过 VPN 漂移的指纹追踪风险，强调了操作系统遥测对隐私的潜在侵扰，以及隐私工具的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://korben.info/desactiver-gdid-windows/desactiver-gdid-windows-1.jpg' data-full='https://korben.info/desactiver-gdid-windows/desactiver-gdid-windows-1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(451) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nycsubway.org/wiki/Subway_Signals%3A_A_Complete_Guide' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NYC Subway Signals: A Complete Guide (2012)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纽约地铁信号：完整指南</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48979823' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48979823' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This comprehensive guide explains NYC subway signaling systems, covering historical development, signal types, aspects, and how they govern train movement and safety. Understanding these signals clarifies why upgrades are complex and costly, and why legacy signaling still shapes operational limits and service patterns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这份详尽指南介绍了纽约地铁的信号系统，包括历史演进、信号类型、显示含义以及它们如何控制列车运行与安全。掌握这些内容能解释升级为何复杂且昂贵，以及为何遗留信号仍然制约运营能力和班次安排。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49063523' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49063523' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fonts In Use catalogs real-world font usage across brands and media, helping designers see type in context and evaluate readability and tone. The site facilitates informed font choices beyond specimen books, with contextual examples.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Fonts In Use 汇集真实世界中的字体使用案例，帮助设计师在实际场景中看到字体的效果、可读性和情感语气。为字体选型提供比单一样张更丰富的参考。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical guide to tracking where to watch titles across streaming services, including tips for licensing and regional availability checks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于跨流媒体平台追踪剧集播出地址的实用指南，包含许可与区域可用性的检查要点。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical, data-driven guide to interpreting paintings: analyze composition, color relationships, brushwork, and historical context. The approach teaches readers to extract narrative and meaning from visual cues, turning art appreciation into a structured skill. It’s a useful framework for educators and enthusiasts alike.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份以数据驱动的实用指南，教你解读绘画：分析构图、色彩关系、笔触及历史语境。通过视觉线索提取叙事与意义，将欣赏艺术的过程变成一项可结构化的技能，适合教育者与爱好者共同提升。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative column argues that AI hype and overreliance erode human oversight in governance and business, urging smarter policy guardrails and accountability. It warns against letting automated systems take the driver seat in critical decisions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇挑衅性的专栏认为人工智能的炒作与过度依赖正在侵蚀治理与商业中的人类监督，主张建立更智能的政策边界与问责机制，并警惕让自动化系统主导关键决策。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48953189' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(460) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.uq.edu.au/2026-07-sensing-warm-and-cool-how-body-detects-temperature-changes' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sensing warm and cool: how the body detects temperature changes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>感知冷热：身体如何检测温度变化</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48953189' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 34</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48953189' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New findings outline molecular and neural pathways that distinguish warming from cooling, implicating distinct thermoreceptors and central circuits that encode temperature change. Better understanding these mechanisms aids treatments for pain, thermoregulatory disorders, and engineering of bioinspired sensors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新研究梳理了区分升温与降温的分子与神经通路，涉及不同的温度感受器和中枢回路用以编码温度变化。深入把握这些机制有助于改善疼痛与体温调节疾病的治疗，也能启发仿生传感器设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.uq.edu.au/sites/default/files/styles/card_image/public/2026-07/hmbs-qbi-temperature_1080.jpg?h=a81fead4&amp;itok=K-nFr_BV' data-full='https://news.uq.edu.au/sites/default/files/styles/card_image/public/2026-07/hmbs-qbi-temperature_1080.jpg?h=a81fead4&amp;itok=K-nFr_BV' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48861496' data-tags='Energy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(461) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/self-powered-trailers-freight-decarbonization' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Self-Powered Trailers Promise Leaner Freight Runs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自供能拖车：实现更省燃料的货运</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48861496' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 34</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48861496' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Self-powered trailers use onboard energy harvesting to power auxiliaries, reducing diesel burn in freight operations and cutting emissions. The approach promises lower lifecycle costs but faces challenges like payload impact, maintenance, and regulatory hurdles before wide adoption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>自供能拖车通过车载能源采集为辅助系统供电，从而在货运中降低柴油消耗和排放。这一思路有望降低总体成本，但在载荷影响、维护以及监管等方面仍面临挑战，尚需时间普及。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=67112537&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C125%2C0%2C125' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=67112537&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C125%2C0%2C125' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968408' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968408' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>

<p class='hn-title'>(471) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.themarshallproject.org/2026/07/15/ice-data-immigration-detention-transparency' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Amid Increased Scrutiny, ICE Detention and Deportation Data Goes Dark</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在加强审查之际，ICE 拘留与遣返数据变成不可见</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48993465' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 27</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48993465' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ICE has pulled back public access or gone dark on detention and deportation data amid scrutiny, reducing transparency around enforcement outcomes. The move draws criticism from watchdogs and could hinder researchers and policymakers trying to assess practices and disparities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在外界监督加强之时，ICE 的拘留与遣返数据变得不再对外可见，透明度下降。此举引发对移民执法问责和基于数据的政策制定的担忧，相关倡议方呼吁恢复公开数据。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(472) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://readthetape.cc/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Read the Tape – Wordle for daytrading, five blind S&amp;P 500 charts a day</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Read the Tape — 日内交易的 Wordle：每日五张盲图的 S&amp;P 500</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48977628' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 27</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48977628' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Read the Tape is a Show HN project that reframes day trading as a Wordle-like puzzle: you’re shown five day-by-day S&amp;P 500 charts with no guidance and must guess the next move. It demonstrates how lightweight, puzzle-oriented data visualization can train intuition without heavy analytics stacks, inviting traders to rethink data consumption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Read the Tape 将日内交易变成一个 Wordle 风格的日常挑战：每天给出五张看不带指引的 S&amp;P 500 图表，参与者猜测后续走势。这个项目展示了用简洁的可视化和游戏化方式训练交易直觉的可能性，降低进入门槛。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://readthetape.cc/card-a9cc65fb.png' data-full='https://readthetape.cc/card-a9cc65fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece surveys motion-sensor-based home security options that avoid cameras, focusing on privacy-preserving devices. It covers products and approaches that alert homeowners to movement or anomalies without continuous video capture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中聚焦以隐私为先的家庭安防方案，依靠运动传感的设备替代摄像头监控。介绍了多款产品与实现方式，能在不持续拍摄视频的情况下实现告警与自动化。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(476) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/jul/26/new-jersey-surveillance-pricing-ban' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New Jersey governor signs law banning surveillance pricing to protect shoppers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新泽西州州长签署法案 禁止以监控数据定价来保护购物者</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49064392' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49064392' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The law blocks pricing that leverages surveillance data to discriminate shoppers, aiming to level the playing field and protect privacy. It signals a broader regulatory push to curb data-driven price discrimination in retail.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新泽西州通过的法案禁止以监控数据进行定价歧视，旨在保护购物者隐私与公平交易。这显示监管在遏制数据驱动零售定价方面的趋势。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A PHP server claims to handle an order of magnitude more concurrent requests than Nginx+PHP-FPM by employing an optimized event-driven architecture and smarter resource management. The README details architecture choices and benchmark results.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个自研 PHP 服务器声称在并发请求数上比 Nginx+PHP-FPM 高出十倍，源于事件驱动架构与高效资源管理。自述中列出架构设计要点与基准测试结果。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The kernel project is adding a form of $ORIGIN support for dynamic library path resolution, enabling more relocatable binaries. The change is partial and comes with caveats about security and compatibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Linux 内核将实现对 $ORIGIN 的部分支持，以改进动态库路径解析和可移植性。此举是部分实现，伴随安全与兼容性方面的注意事项。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>W4ME Station ships a WASM-4 runtime compatible with Java ME devices, letting modern WASM-based games run on feature phones. It demonstrates how compact runtimes can bridge old hardware with contemporary web-like experiences.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(487) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49057972' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ask HN: What are the most promising RL fields for a new master student?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ask HN：对于新硕士生，最有前途的强化学习领域是什么？</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A thread asks which RL subfields look most promising for a new master&#x27;s student, pointing to model-based, offline RL and robotics as high-demand areas. It also touches safe RL, data efficiency, and generalization as practical considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>HN 讨论贴聚焦新硕士生在强化学习领域的前景，模型基、离线 RL 与机器人等方向被视为高需求领域。其他热点包括安全性、泛化与数据效率，为选题提供实用参考。</p>
